### Lines shared with other tablets
- **VII 28–29 = VI 128–129** (two-line speech formula): `אנקידו פתח את פיו ואמר, / דיבר אל גילגמש:`, here with VII's own brackets (`אֶנְקִידוּ [פתח] את פ[יו ואמר],` / `דיבר [אל גִּלְגָּמֶשׁ]:`).
- **VII 44–45 → V 316–317** (not yet translated; first copy here): `שישה קנים גּוֹבְהֵךְ, שני קנים רוֹחְבֵּךְ, [אמה] אחת [עוֹבְיֵךְ],` / `עמודך, צירך ו*פּוֹתֵךְ* – מאחד הם.` In V the pronouns are 3rd person (*mēlûša*, *rupussu*, *šukûša* …), V 316 begins with *ēpuš-ma dalta* "I made a door", and *ištēt ammat uppuša* "one cubit its thickness" stands in V 317: V should copy the Hebrew word for word and change only that.
- **VII 65–67, 92**: the tears formula (`מהר, חיש, זולגות דמעותיו`; `זולגות דמעותיו` as VI 83). **VII 68**: the one-line speech formula. **VII 90**: the dawn formula (`כשהאיר אור הבוקר`). **VII 148**: `שמע X את דבר Y`. **VII 165 ~ I 244**: `החלום אשר ראיתי בלילה הזה`, with *mimmû* `מה` for *ummī*. **VII 255**: `יום אחד … יום שני`.
- **VII 91–92 ~ VI 82–83, V 90–91, 185–186**: `לפני X … זולגות דמעותיו/דמעותיה`; V should use `נשא גילגמש את ראשו, לפני שמש בוכה,` / `לפני קרני שַׁמַשׁ זולגות דמעותיו:` (V 90–91 have *ibakki*, VII *unabbi* `מילל`).
- **VII 94 = I 111** + *šū*: `[הוא?] – צייד, אדם לוכד,`.
- **VII 102 = 151** (brackets only).
- **VII 132 → IV 194** (`שמש שמע את מאמר פיו,`), **VII 133 → V 96** (`מיד – תרועה מן השמים צועקת וצועקת אליו:`; V's own brackets).
- **VII 135–136 ~ VI 27–28**: `לחם הראוי לאֵלוּת`, `סובא מובחר … הראוי למלכות`, as fixed in VI.
- **VII 139–147 → VIII 84–91** (first copy here; VIII in the 1st person): VIII 84 `אשכיב אותך במשכב גדול,`, 85 `במשכב של חיבה אשכיב אותך,`, 86 `אושיב אותך במשכן מנוחה, משכן השמאל,`, 87 `נסיכי הקרקע ינשקו את רגליך,`, 88 `אַבְכֶּה עליך את אנשי אורוך, אַאֲבִיל אותם עליך,`, 89 `את האנשים השופעים אמלא עליך עמל.`, 90 `ואני – אחריך אַשִּׂיא את גוויתי פרע,`, 91 `לבשתי עור לביא, ומשוטט אני בשדה.` (VIII 91 *altabbiš … arappud*; cf. X 62 `וּמְשׁוֹטֵט אֲנִי בַּשָּׂדֶה`).
- **VII 147 ~ X 45** (fixed passage §3.2): `ומשוטט … בשדה`.
- **VII 182 ~ VI 61, 76**: *târu* D = `הפך`.
- **VII 184–192 → Ištar's Descent 4–11, Nergal and Ereškigal c+1–c+7** (not in this corpus): the Hebrew fixed here should bind a future translation of those texts.
- **VII 193 = 198** (brackets only).
- **VII 251 ~ X 55–56, 132–133, 232–233; OB VA+BM**: `אשר עמך עברתי את כל התלאות` (1st person of the fixed passage).
- **VII 256 ~ XI 245** (Jensen's restoration *ašib mūtu*, not translated).
- `verify.py` reports no differences: eBL's exact parallels for VII point only to the MB/Hittite and other-text chapters.

### Proposals for the shared renderings
1. **Key terms (§4):** *arāru* infinitive = `לְאָרֵר` (Piel; the §4 row's `לָאֹר` is a plene homograph of "to the light"); confirm that VII 101 is the only *arāru* of VII (all other curses *nazāru*/*ezēru* = `גִּדֵּף`). *zêru* "hate" = `שָׂנֵא` (VII 61, 263; eBL lemmatizes *ezēru*). *ezēbu* = `עָזַב` (+ N `נֶעֱזַב`) as a key verb (VII 75–76, 161 ↔ Gen 2:24). *eṣēru* D stative = `צוּיְּרוּ` (VII 88). *imtu* = `חֵמָה` [cognate] (VII 175). *ēdēnu* = `לְבַד` (VII 163 ↔ Gen 2:18). *bīt eklēti* = `בֵּית הַחֲשֵׁכָה`; *bīt epri* = `בֵּית הֶעָפָר`; *tayyāru* = `שָׁב` (root *târu*); *ēribu* = `בָּא` (`בָּאָיו`); *bubūtu* = `מָזוֹן`. *baltu* / *ašāgu* = `חוֹחַ` / `חֵדֶק` (never `קוֹץ` / `דַּרְדַּר`); *paḫāru* = `קַדָּר` (never `יוֹצֵר`). *uznu* "ear/understanding" note at every occurrence. *labāšu* D = `הִלְבִּישׁ` (VII 137) added to the Gen 3:21 rows.
2. **Grammar:** Gtn iteratives never with `שוב ושוב` (the root of *târu*): `הִקְשִׁיב` (*šemû* Gtn), doubling (`צועקת וצועקת`), or `פעם אחר פעם`; align VI 84 (open decision 2).
3. **Collision to fix in Tablet I:** I 57 renders *uqnâti* `[תְּכֵלֶת]`, while VII 127 has *takiltu*, the real cognate of `תְּכֵלֶת`; I 57 should become `[לזורית]` (as `../renderings.md` §4 *uqnû* = `לָזוּרִית`).
4. **Ordinary words (§5):** *daltu* `דֶּלֶת` (already); *ḫalbu* = `חֹרֶשׁ`; *nasāqu* = `בָּרַר`; *nindanu* = `קָנֶה`; *šukû* = `עַמּוּד`; *sāḫiru* = `צִיר`; *šagammu* = `*פּוֹת*`; *retû* = `קָבַע`; *amu* = `רַפְסוֹדָה`; *zaqāpu* = `זָקַף`; *nērebu* = `מָבוֹא`; *gimillu* = `גְּמוּל`; *šūquru* = `יְקַר־עֵרֶךְ`, *aqru* = `יָקָר`; *pirittu* = `בְּעָתָה`; *zubbu* = `זְבוּב`; *ḫawû* = `הָמָה`; *ṣullû* = `הִתְחַנֵּן`, *suppû* = `הִפְצִיר`; *utnennu* = `תְּחִנָּה`; *nubbû* = `יִלֵּל`; *deššu* = `יָתֵד`; *šarūru* = `קֶרֶן (אוֹר)`; *nēmelu* = `רֶוַח`; *idū* = `מַשְׂכֹּרֶת`; *ḫalāqu* D = `אִבֵּד`; *zittu* = `חֵלֶק`; *ḫarṣu* = `גָּרוּעַ`; *apāniš* = `בַּחַלּוֹן`; *dinnû* = `יָצוּעַ`; *dakkannu* = `*סַפְסָל*`; *išpallurtu* = `פָּרָשַׁת (דְּרָכִים)`; *ḫurbu* = `חָרְבָּה`; *maṣallu* = `מְלוּנָה`; *manzāzu* = `מַעֲמָד`; *qalāpu* = `קָלַף`; *ṣamû* = `צָמֵא`; *šakru* = `שִׁכּוֹר`; *tarbuʾu* = `אָבָק`; *bēlet dīni* = `בַּעֲלַת דִּין`; *sêru* = `טָח`; *itinnu* = `בַּנַּאי`; *ūru* "roof" = `גַּג`; *qadû* = `יַנְשׁוּף`; *rabāṣu* = `רָבַץ`; *qerītu* = `מִשְׁתֶּה`; *takiltu* = `תְּכֵלֶת`; *sūnu* = `חֲלָצַיִם`; *sehû* D = `חִלֵּל`; *tukku* = `תְּרוּעָה`; *teknû* = `חִבָּה`; *nēḫtu* = `מְנוּחָה`; *malku* = `נָסִיךְ`; *bakû* Š = `הִבְכָּה`; *damāmu* Š = `הֶאֱבִיל`; *našû* Š = `הִשִּׂיא`; *aggu* = `קוֹצֵף`, *ezzu* = `זוֹעֵם`; *šakkanakku* = `מוֹשֵׁל`; *rēdû* = `חַיָּל`; *miserru* = `מֵזַח`; *paṭāru* = `הִתִּיר`; *ṣurru* = `צוֹר`; *inṣabtu* = `נֶזֶם`; *nidinnu* = `מַתָּנָה`; *ganūnu* = `מַחְסָן`; *išpiku* = `אָסָם`; *šapku* = `שָׁפוּךְ`; *wabālu* Št = `הִרְהֵר`; *ukkulu* = `מֻחְשָׁךְ`; *rittu* = `כַּף`; *ṣupru* = `צִפֹּרֶן`; *danānu* D = `חָזַק עַל`; *keppû* = `חֶבֶל־קְפִיצָה`; *ṭebû* D = `הִטְבִּיעַ`; *raḫāṣu* I = `רָמַס`; *šabāḫu* = `שָׁקַע`; *kamāsu* I = `כִּנֵּס`; *bêlu* = `מָשַׁל`; *šumû* = `צְלִי`; *ultu ūmē pāna* = `מִימֵי קֶדֶם`; *ṭupšarru(tu)* = `סוֹפֵר(ת)`; *kimaḫḫu* = `קֶבֶר`; *murṣu* = `חֳלִי`; *gamāru* = `גָּמַר`; *qerbu* "midst" = `תּוֹךְ` beside *qablu* = `קְרָב`.
5. **Names (§1):** Irkalla `אִירְכַּלָּה` used as both place and goddess (note); priest titles transliterated with `כּוֹהֵן` (`כּוֹהֲנֵי־אֵן`, `־לַגָּר`, `־לוּמַחּוּ`, `־גוּדַפְּסוּ`; *išippu* `כּוֹהֵן־טָהֳרָה`).

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A reserved root can enter through an adverb**: the Gtn iterative rendered `שוב ושוב` puts the root of *târu* (Gen 3:19 `עַד שׁוּבְךָ`) into lines that have no *târu*; grep reserved roots in adverbs and idioms too, not only in verbs.
- **One eBL lemma can cover three verbs** (*ezēru* for *nazāru* "curse", *ezēru* proper and *zêru* "hate"): parse the form (hollow vs strong root) before deciding which Hebrew verb – and therefore which Gen 3 contact – a line gets.
- **A coordinator's speech grouping should be checked against the section breaks and the content** (VII 84–89 is Enkidu's reply, not Gilgameš's answer); record the attribution as an open decision when no formula survives.
- **Stock passages shared with other compositions** (VII 184–192 = Ištar's Descent 4–11) should be fixed once, in the first translated copy, and listed for the corpus that will translate the other text.

## Tablet X

### Scope
SB Tablet X, all 325 eBL labels (1–294, 292′–294′, 295–322): Šiduri the ale-wife at the sea-shore (1–4); Gilgameš arrives in a pelt, she bars the gate and goes up on the roof (5–18); the threat to the door and the exchange (19–39); the question and the lament, first copy (40–71); "what is the road to Ūta-napišti?" – no crossing, only Šamaš, the Waters of Death, Ur-šanabi and the Stone Ones (72–91); Gilgameš smashes the Stone Ones (92–112); the question and the lament, second copy (113–148); the punting-poles (149–172); the crossing of the Waters of Death, twelve poles, the garment as sail (173–183); Ūta-napišti sees the boat, "Long live Ūta-napišti" (184–211); the third copy (212–248); Gilgameš's account of his wanderings and the gate of sorrow (249–265); Ūta-napišti's speech: the fool's lot, the king's duties, the moon and the wakeful gods (266–294′), and the speech on death (295–322). Manuscripts: Nineveh NinNA1a (K₁), NinNA1c (K₃), K 8579 (K₂); Kalḫu KalNA1 (z); Babylon BabNB1 (f, + BM 36257) and the Parthian BabPar2 (b, + BM 34306); l. 1 also as the IX catch-line (NinNAQuo1). No manuscript at all for 47–60, 126–130 (and the copies are fully broken in 61–62, 136, 139, 142, 146, 148, 234, 238); single-manuscript stretches 47–211 (NinNA1a, KalNA1 for 196–211); 280–294 BabPar2 only.

### Sources consulted
- eBL `L/1/4` SB X (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-SB-X/reading-edition.md`: N, T, L, notes, parallels, ATF; `manuscripts.md`).
- eBL OB VA+BM (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-OB-VA-BM/reading-edition.md`) for the OB parallels (not translated; cited in the apparatus).
- `../renderings.md` incl. §9–§11 (binding; §3.2 copied in plene spelling), Tablets I, VI, VII and XI 209–328 (`annotated-*.rtl.md`), flood XI 1–208 and its renderings (*peḫû* = `סָתַם`, *kupru* = `כֹּפֶר`, *iṭṭû* = `חֵמָר`, *parīsu*), creation corpus renderings (*mê mūti*, *ḫepû* = `בִּקַּע`, *awû* = `שָׂח`, *sakātu*), law corpus (*maḫāṣu*, *kīma* = `כְּ`, *bēlu* = `בַּעַל`).
- `../parallels_for.py X --all` (177 lines with parallels).
- George's translation (eBL T), after drafting; Tchernichovsky 1924 Tablet X (`../../sources/Ben-Yehuda--Tchernichovsky-1924/full-work--all-tablets.rtl.md`, ll. 2827–3409) after pass (c).
- The vocalized Bible through `../../tools/check_bible_quotes.py --show`.

### Decisions
1. **The files are generated from one data file** (scratchpad `X-part*.txt`: Hebrew, apparatus and footnote per label; the normalization is filled in from eBL by script), so the annotated `>` lines and the clean translation are identical by construction (guidelines §5, "generate … from one line-by-line data file").
2. ***sābītu* = `בַּעֲלַת־הַמִּסְבָּאָה`** (vocalized at 1), always with the maqaf, also in the vocative (73, 258). Reason: *sābītu* is from *sabû* "brew/sell beer" ~ Hebrew סבא (`סֹבֶא`, already *sīrāšu* in `../renderings.md` §4; `זוֹלֵל וְסֹבֵא`); the later `מִסְבָּאָה` keeps the root and the trade. Rejected: `הַמּוֹזֶגֶת` (hides the root), `הַסּוֹבְאָה` ("drunkard"), `פּוּנְדְּקָאִית` (OB: she brews and sells beer).
3. **The fixed passage §3.2 (40–71, 113–148, 213–248) is copied in plene spelling**, as every `>` line: the table's vocalized forms stripped by the strip-test give `זיווך`, `זיווי`, `פניי`, `עליי`, `במעיי`, `מאוד`, `שישה`, `המוות`, `באורח`, `אשתוק`, `אֶדּוֹם` (vocalized: unvocalized "אדום" = red), `שׂוּמִים` (vocalized: "שומים" = garlic) – the only differences from the table's consonants (checked by script). Each copy brackets by its own manuscripts; 47–60 and 126–130 are fully bracketed (no manuscript of that copy). `(למה)` of 42 is kept in all copies. Quotation marks open at 47, 120, 220 and close at 71, 148, 248.
4. **First copies fixed here** (bind later tablets): 31–34 / 128–131 / 228–231 → VIII 52–54 (`אשר נסמכנו (יחד) ועלינו אל ההר,` / `שור השמים אחזנו, ושור השמים קטלנו,` / `השמדנו את חומבבה, אשר ביער הארזים יושב,` / `במבואות ההרים הרגנו אריות.`); 53–54 → VIII 50–51; 62 → IX 5 (`פחדתי מן המוות, ומשוטט אני בשדה.`); 93–94 → IX 15–16 (`נשא גרזן בזרועו,` / `שלף חרב מחגורו,`); 96 → IX 17 (`כחץ אל ביניהם נפל,`); 171 → IV 4, 37, 82, 123 (`מהלך ירח וחצי – ביום השלישי …`); 259–260 → VIII 16–17 (animal names).
5. ***matīma* = `אִי־פַּעַם`** (79, 270), because `מֵעוֹלָם` is reserved; ***im-matīma … im-matīma*** (308–312) = `פעם – … / פעם – …` ("sometimes … sometimes"), which keeps the list-of-times structure (↔ Qoh 3).
6. **The Waters of Death and the crossing:** *mê mūti* = `מֵי הַמָּוֶת` (creation corpus); *šūt abnī* = `אַנְשֵׁי הָאֶבֶן` (§1); *ḫepû* D = `בִּקַּע`, *ḫuppû* = `מְבֻקָּעִים` (creation corpus); *tabāku* = `יָצַק` (flood shared, against XI 316's `נָטַשׁ` "by sense": here the stones are "poured" into the river); *peḫû* = `סָתַם` (flood XI); *nēberu* = `מַעְבָּר`, *nēbertu* = `מַעְבָּרָה`; *pašāqu* G/Š = `צָרָה` / `מוּצֶרֶת`; *parāku* = `חוֹסֵם`; *parīsu* = `מוֹט` (XI 276; flood XI 65 has `מוט־דחיפה`); *nindanu* = `קָנֶה`; *kapāru* "trim" = `קִצֵּעַ` (not `קָלַף` = *qalāpu*; no link with כפר / *kupru*); *tulû* "boss" = `דַּד`; *lapātu* = `נָגַע` (175 ↔ Gen 3:3); *magillu* = `סִירָה`, *elippu* = `סְפִינָה`; *karû* (183) = `תּוֹרֶן` (eBL's lemma "grain heap" does not fit; `כְּרִי` kept for the grain heap); *kappu* = `כָּנָף` also for the arms (101, 183).
7. **Gilgameš's equipment:** *namṣaru* = `חֶרֶב` (§5); *šibbu* "sword-belt" = `חֲגוֹר` (2 Sam 20:8 `חֲגוֹר חֶרֶב`) – a different word from Gen 3:7 `חֲגֹרֹת`, but the root חגר is also *ezēḫu* (I): see proposals. *šalāpu* = `שָׁלַף` [cognate]; *ḫalālu* = `הִתְגַּנֵּב`; *šiltāḫu* = `חֵץ`; *namrīru* = `נֹגַהּ`; *raʾābu* = `רָעַד`, *šâbu* = `רָטַט`.
8. **Šiduri's house:** *kannu* = `כַּן` [cognate] (`כִּיּוֹר … וְכַנּוֹ`, Exod 30:18; vocalized `כַּנִּים`); *kutummu kuttumu* = `בכיסויים מכוסה`; the restored *kulūlu kullulu* = `[בעטיפות? עטופה?]` – not `צָעִיף` (Gen 24:65; 38:14), which would put a Genesis word in a lacuna; *edēlu* = `נָעַל`; *ūru* "roof" = `גַּג` (VII); *nagāšu* Gtn = `תָּעָה` (rapādu has `שׁוֹטֵט`, `נָד` is reserved); *sapannu* = `שְׁפֵלָה`.
9. **Speech and thought:** *uštamma ana libbīša amāta iqabbi* = `שׂחה אל לבה, דבר אומרת,` (11; 185 with *izzakkar* = `דבר מדבר`), *awû* Št = `שָׂח` (creation corpus); *malāku* Gt = `נִמְלַךְ`; *ramānu* = `עַצְמִי`. *mindē-ma* = `אולי` (CDA), against George's "for sure" (13).
10. **The road and its sign:** *ḫarrānu* = `אֹרַח` (73, 150: `מהו אורח אותנפישתי?`), *ittu* = `אוֹת` (74–75, 151–152: `האות שלה`, not `אותה`, which reads "her"). 151 *idna* (NinNA1a writes *id-ni*, the feminine of 74): masculine `תן`. The formula *šumma naṭū-ma / šumma lā naṭū-ma* = `אם ראוי – … / ואם לא ראוי – …` (§2) with *lūbir* = `אעבורה`, *lurpud* = `אשוטטה`, *ebir* = `עבור`, *iḫis* = `הִסּוֹג` (*naḫāsu* = `נָסוֹג`, §10).
11. **Šamaš and the sun:** `שַׁמַשׁ` (god, 81–82) and `שֶׁמֶשׁ` (*šamšu*, the sun, 314) vocalized every time (both occur, `../renderings.md` §1). *qurādu* = `גִּבּוֹר` (↔ Ps 19:6).
12. **The wanderings (249–265):** *umma* = `(אמרתי) כך:` with the inner quotation in `'…'`; *saḫāru* = `חָזַר` (`חזרתי, הלכתי`, "go around" – George "again"); *etēqu* Gtn = `חלפתי פעם אחר פעם`, *ebēru* Gtn = `עברתי פעם אחר פעם` (no `שוב ושוב`, §11); *šittu ṭābtu* = `שינה טובה` (`טוב` for *ṭābu*); *nazāqu* Š (eBL: *esēqu*) = `ייסרתי את עצמי`; *dalāpu* = `עֵר`, *ina dalāpi* = `בְּעֵרוּת` (vocalized; "בבערות"), *taddalip* = `עַרְתָּ`; *šerʾānu* = `גִּיד`; *kašāru* Gt = `השיג`; *mānaḫtu* = `יְגִיעַ`; *ana lēt* = `אל צד` (by sense; *lētu* = `לְחִי` elsewhere); *lubuštu* = `לְבוּשׁ`; *mindinu* = `*בַּרְדְּלָס*`; *ṭabāḫu* D "flay" = `הִפְשִׁיט` (Lev 1:6); *kupru u ittû* = `בכּוֹפֶר ובחֵמָר` (vocalized `כּוֹפֶר`, plene of `כֹּפֶר`); *mēlulu* = `מָחוֹל`; *ḫaddû* = `שָׂמֵחַ` (I 232).
13. **Ūta-napišti's speech (266–322):** *ballu* (*balālu*) = `בָּלוּל`; *lillu* = `כְּסִיל`; *nadû* (a throne) = `השליכו` (literal, as I 100, VII 88); *puḫru* = `אֲסֵפָה`; *šuršummu* = `שְׁמָרִים`; *ḫimētu* = `חֶמְאָה` [cognate]; *gunnu* = `*גס*`; *tuḫḫu* = `סובין`, *kukkušu* = `*קמח גרוע*`; *mašḫantu* = `*סחבה*`; *nēbeḫu* = `אַבְנֵט`, *ebēḫu* = `חָגַר`; *milku* = `עֵצָה`; *rēša našû* = `נשא ראש` (↔ Gen 40:13); *ēru lā sākipu* = `ער, לא נם`; *ultu pān šakin* = `מִלְּפָנִים שׂוּם`; *kapādu* = `חֲשׁוֹב`; *tappûtu* = `חֲבֵרוּת`; *zāninu* = `מְכַלְכֵּל` (↔ Gen 45:11; 47:12); *ištarātu* = `הָאֵלוֹת` (vocalized: "האלות" = the curses); *qīštu* = `תְּשׁוּרָה` (apart from *nidinnu* = `מַתָּנָה`); *qerēbu* D = `קֵרֵב` (300 ↔ Gen 47:29); *qanû* = `קָנֶה`, *apu* = `אֲגַם`; *ḫaṣābu* / *ḫāṣipu* = `נגדע` / `גודע` (301, 307; George 2003 reads one verb, eBL lemmatizes 307 as *ḫasāpu*); *šumu* = `שֵׁם` (George "progeny", noted); *šalālu* = `שָׁלַל`, *šallu* = `שָׁלוּל`; *aggu* = `קוֹצֵף`; *bītu* … *epēšu* = `עושים בית`; *qinna qanānu* = `מקננים קן` (`../renderings.md` §4, cognate row); *zâzu* = `חָלַק`; *zērūtu* = `שִׂנְאָה`; *mīlu* = `גֵּאוּת` (not the deluge); *našû* intransitive = `נִשָּׂא`; *kulīlu* = `שַׁפִּירִית`; *neqelpû* = `צָף`; *eṣēru* = `צָר` with *ṣalmu* = `צֶלֶם` (317, as I 49); *lullû* = `קַדְמוֹן` (318); *karābu* = `בֵּרֵךְ` "greet"; *paḫāru* G = `הִתְקַבֵּץ`; *bānât šīmti* = `בּוֹנַת הַגּוֹרָל` (*banû* = `בָּנָה`, never `בָּרָא` / `יוֹצֶרֶת`); *šīmta šâmu* = `גורל גזרה`; *šakānu* = `שׂמו` (321); *uddû* = `הוֹדִיעוּ` (322, *edû* D = `ידע`), *ūmīšu* plural = `ימיו` (George "the day").
14. **Names** vocalized at first occurrence in the tablet: שִׁידוּרִי (1), גִּלְגָּמֶשׁ (5), אֶנְקִידוּ (30), חוּמְבָּבָה (33, restored), אוּתְנַפִּשְׁתִּי (73), שַׁמַשׁ (81–82), אוּרְשַׁנַבִּי (87), אוּבָּר־טוּטוּ (208), אָנוּ (267), סִין (281), אֲנוּנָּכִּי (319), מַמִּיתוּם (320; unvocalized it reads like "killing them"). `רֵעִי` always vocalized; `אֵלִים` vocalized without the article (7, 268); `שֵׁב` (271) vocalized (unvocalized "שב" = "returned", the root of *târu*).
15. **Speech marks follow the speakers, not George's quotation marks**: no new quotation at 257, 274, 286 (George's T opens or closes there inside one speech); Ūta-napišti's speech 266–293 is closed at 293 and reopened at 293′ (as George), running to 322.
16. **eBL slips recorded in the apparatus:** 35 N *ana šâši* for the manuscript's *ana šâšu* (to him); 157 *tuḫtappi* lemmatized as the adjective *ḫepû* I; 176–179 ordinals lemmatized as cardinals and other words ("fifty", "one-sixth", "eighty", "ninety", "20th day"); 183 *karâ* as *karû* "grain heap"; 216, 223 *arḫi* as *warḫu* "month" and *mašlū* as *mašlu* "half"; 255 *uštēziq* as *esēqu*; 259 *asa* as *asu* "myrtle"; 263 *kupri* as *gupru* "shepherd's hut"; 278 *īši* as *išû* (probably the imperative *iši*); 293 T "throw down" against the lemma *nadānu*; *ṣēru* "back" throughout. eBL's parallels field pairs many lines of the threefold refrain with the wrong neighbour (e.g. X 47 = X 40, 241 = 143, 145 = 69, I 118 = X 9): see "Lines shared".

### Open decisions for the user
1. *sābītu* = `בַּעֲלַת־הַמִּסְבָּאָה` (chosen; keeps the cognate root סבא), or the shorter `הַמּוֹזֶגֶת` / `הַפּוּנְדְּקָאִית`?
2. The fixed passage in plene spelling: `זיווך`, `זיווי` (the table's `זִיוְךָ` strips to "זיוך") – confirm the plene forms for all copies (and for VIII later)?
3. *matīma* = `אִי־פַּעַם` (modern-sounding) in 79 and 270, or `מֵעוֹדִי`-type periphrases (`מעולם` is reserved)?
4. *ṭabāḫu* D "flay" (261) = `מַפְשִׁיט` (natural; Lev 1:6), although פשט also renders *šaḫāṭu* "take off (a garment)" (I) – or the cognate `טבח` (not idiomatic for skins)?
5. *šibbu* (94, 165) = `חֲגוֹר` (2 Sam 20:8) – the root חגר also renders *ezēḫu* (I); alternative `מֵזַח` is taken (VII *miserru*).
6. *šumu* (301) = `שֵׁם` (chosen, cognate, ↔ Gen 6:4; 11:4) against George's "progeny" (`זַרְעָהּ` would hide the cognate).
7. *ūmīšu* (322) = `ימיו` (plural, as the manuscripts' UD-MEŠ) or George's singular `יומו` (closer to Gen 27:2 `יוֹם מוֹתִי`)?
8. *nadû* "set down (a throne)" (271) = `השליכו` (literal, corpus rule) or `הציבו` by sense?
9. *mindē-ma* (13) = `אולי` (CDA) or George's `אכן` ("for sure")?
10. 263: the restoration *lipḫû bābša* "may they seal its door" is George's; the line's Hebrew brackets it all (`[יסתמו? את שערה?]`), so the Gen 6:14 parallel rests on *kupru* only (preserved).

| 312 | nāru iššâ mīla ubla | הנהר נִשָּׂא, הביא גאות | `וַיִּרְבּוּ הַמַּיִם וַיִּשְׂאוּ אֶת הַתֵּבָה וַתָּרָם מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ` (בראשית ז:יז) | [divergence] | *našû* = נשא [cognate]; *mīlu* = the seasonal high water (`גאות`), not the deluge (*abūbu*) – the flood hero speaks of ordinary floods. |
| 313–315 | kulīlu … ultu ullânum-ma ul ibašši mimma | שפירית … ומיד – אין מאומה | `וְאֵינֶנּוּ כִּי לָקַח אֹתוֹ אֱלֹהִים` (בראשית ה:כד) | [equivalent] | Sudden disappearance ("and he was not"); [divergence] Enoch is taken, the mayfly vanishes. Also `כִּי רוּחַ עָבְרָה-בּוֹ וְאֵינֶנּוּ` (תהלים קג:טז). |
| 317 | ša mūti ul iṣṣirū ṣalamšu | של המוות – לא צרו את צלמו | `וַיִּבְרָא אֱלֹהִים אֶת הָאָדָם בְּצַלְמוֹ בְּצֶלֶם אֱלֹהִים בָּרָא אֹתוֹ` (בראשית א:כז) | [cognate] | **Key.** *ṣalmu* = צלם: man has an "image", death has none. Also `וַיּוֹלֶד בִּדְמוּתוֹ כְּצַלְמוֹ` (בראשית ה:ג). |
| 317 | iṣṣirū | צרו | `וַיִּיצֶר יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים אֶת הָאָדָם` (בראשית ב:ז) | [cognate] | *eṣēru* ~ יצר (rendered `צָר`): with *ṣalmu*, both words of Gen 1:27 and 2:7 in one line, as I 49 (Gilgameš's creation) – here negated, of death. |

| 320 | bānât šīmti | בונת הגורל | `וַיִּבֶן יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים אֶת הַצֵּלָע` (בראשית ב:כב) | [equivalent] | *banû* = בנה (never ברא/יצר): fate is "built", as Aruru "built" Enkidu (I 101); [absent] no goddess of fate in Genesis. |

OB VA+BM a+18–20: "for mankind they set death, life they kept in their own hands". |
| 321 | mūta u balāṭa | מוות וחיים | `הַחַיִּים וְהַמָּוֶת נָתַתִּי לְפָנֶיךָ הַבְּרָכָה וְהַקְּלָלָה וּבָחַרְתָּ בַּחַיִּים` (דברים ל:יט) | [equivalent] | The same pair; the order reversed. |

| 322 | ul uddû ūmīšu | לא הודיעו את ימיו | `הוֹדִיעֵנִי יְהוָה קִצִּי וּמִדַּת יָמַי מַה-הִיא` (תהלים לט:ה) | [cognate] | Same verb (Hiphil = D) and object ("my days"): the psalmist asks for what the gods withheld. |
| 322 | ul uddû | לא הודיעו | `כִּי גַּם לֹא יֵדַע הָאָדָם אֶת עִתּוֹ` (קהלת ט:יב) | [cognate] | Man does not know his time. |

| 322 | ul uddû | לא הודיעו | `וִהְיִיתֶם כֵּאלֹהִים יֹדְעֵי טוֹב וָרָע` (בראשית ג:ה) | [equivalent] | Knowledge kept by the gods (*edû* = ידע). |

### Lines shared with other tablets
- **The fixed passage §3.2**: 40–71 = 113–148 = 213–248 (vocative and speaker only; each with its own brackets). Plene spelling: decision 3. 42/115/215 = I 118 in the 2nd person with `(למה)`; 43/116/216 = I 119 in the singular.
- **X 8–9 ~ I 118–119**: `יש יגון ב[מעיו],` / `להולך דרך רחוקה משולים פניו.` (singular *urḫi rūqati*, as the fixed passage; I 119 has the plural `דרכים רחוקות`).
- **X 92, 163 = XI 287**: `גילגמש – כשמעו את זאת,` (163 `[כשמעו]`); also binding for VIII 211, IX 136.
- **X 169–170 = XI 271–272**: `גילגמש ואורשנבי רכבו על [הספינה],` / `את הסירה השליכו (למים), הם – [רכבו] (עליה).` (own brackets).
- **X 212, 266 = XI 8** (`אותנפישתי דיבר אליו, אל גילגמש:`, own brackets).
- **Fixed here for tablets not yet translated**: VIII 50–54 (= X 53–54, 31–34 / 128–131); VIII 16–17 (animal names of 259–260: `דֹּב, צָבוֹעַ, אַרְיֵה, נָמֵר, *בַּרְדְּלָס*`, `אַיָּל, יָעֵל`); IX 5 (= 62); IX 15–17 (= 93–94, 96); IX 49 (~ 7, `בשר אֵלִים`); IX 126 (= 44/51 type); IV 4, 37, 82, 123 (~ 171 `מהלך ירח וחצי – ביום השלישי …`); OB VA+BM a+1–a+10 (~ 55–60), a+39–a+41 (~ 76, 78–79), b+1 (~ 106), b+7 (~ 109), b+21–b+28 (~ 155–161), a+18–a+20 (~ 321: `שׂמו … מוות`).
- **`verify.py` differences (62), all justified**: (a) eBL's "=" between a line and its negated or vocative counterpart in the refrain (40/47, 41/48, 42/49, 43/50, 44/51, 45/52, 113/120 … 218/225, 224/125, 51/42, 51/122, 122/115) – different Akkadian (*lā*, person); (b) eBL pairs shifted by one line (I 118 = X 9/42/49/115/122/215/222 and I 119 = X 10/43/…: the true pairs are I 118 ~ X 8, I 119 ~ X 9; X 99/164, 110/164, 145/69, 245/144, 246/144, 241/143, 243/64, 63/242, 65/240, 240/142, 242/140) – unrelated lines; (c) with or without the name Enkidu (126/127, 127/53, 133/55, 233/55, 227/226) or the second *alâ* (32/129, 32/229) or a different person/gender (32/39, 74/151, 152/75, 106/157) or addressee (46/119) – the Akkadian differs; (d) the opening quotation mark only (40/113, 40/213, 120/213, 121/214, 214/221, 215/222 …, listed under "brackets/marks only" when the brackets are the only difference).

### Proposals for the shared renderings
1. **Names / epithets (§1):** *sābītu* = `בַּעֲלַת־הַמִּסְבָּאָה` (always with maqaf; vocative too); Mammītum always vocalized `מַמִּיתוּם` (add to §7 "always vocalize": unvocalized it reads "killing them"); `אָנוּ`, `סִין` vocalized (unvocalized `אנו` = "we", `סין` = "China"); `שֵׁב` (imperative of ישב) vocalized wherever *târu*'s `שב` could be read.
2. **Key terms (§4):** *mê mūti* = `מֵי הַמָּוֶת` (confirm for the corpus); *šūt abnī* = `אַנְשֵׁי הָאֶבֶן` (used); *qinna qanānu* = `מקננים קן` (X 309 – done); *ḫimētu* = `חֶמְאָה` [cognate]; *zāninu* = `מְכַלְכֵּל`; *ṣalmu* + *eṣēru* (X 317) added to the Gen 1:27 / 2:7 rows; *šumu* "name/progeny" = `שֵׁם` always; *kupru u ittû* = `כֹּפֶר וְחֵמָר` (flood XI 66–67, X 263).
3. **Ordinary words (§5):** *matīma* = `אִי־פַּעַם`, *im-matīma* = `פַּעַם`; *edēlu* = `נָעַל`; *nagāšu* = `תָּעָה`; *kannu* = `כַּן`; *sapannu* = `שְׁפֵלָה`; *awû* Št = `שָׂח`; *zuqtu* = `סַנְטֵר`; *šaqû* D = `הִגְבִּיהַּ`; *šalāpu* = `שָׁלַף`; *šibbu* = `חֲגוֹר` (open decision 5); *ḫalālu* = `הִתְגַּנֵּב`; *šiltāḫu* = `חֵץ`; *šapû* (noise) = `הִתְעַבָּה`; *raʾābu* = `רָעַד`, *šâbu* = `רָטַט`; *ḫepû* D = `בִּקַּע`; *tabāku* (stones into a river) = `יָצַק`; *irtu* = `חָזֶה`; *urnu* = `אֹרֶן` [cognate]; *qatāpu* = `קָטַף` [cognate]; *pašāqu* = `צַר`, Š `מוּצָר`; *parāku* = `חָסַם`; *nēbertu* = `מַעְבָּרָה` (≠ *nēberu* `מַעְבָּר`); *kapāru* = `קִצֵּעַ`; *tulû* = `דַּד`; *nindanu* (measure) = `קָנֶה`; *ḫamāṣu* = `תָּלַשׁ`; *karû* (mast) = `תּוֹרֶן`; *qablu* "hips" = `מָתְנַיִם`; *šapattu* = "half-month" (by sense, `חֲצִי`; false friend `שַׁבָּת`); *etēqu* = `חָלַף`; *šerʾānu* = `גִּיד`; *nazāqu* Š = `יִסֵּר`; *kašāru* = `הִשִּׂיג`; *asu* = `דֹּב`; *mindinu* = `*בַּרְדְּלָס*`; *ṭabāḫu* D "flay" = `הִפְשִׁיט` (open decision 4); *mēlulu* = `מָחוֹל`; *lillu* = `כְּסִיל`; *šuršummu* = `שְׁמָרִים`; *tuḫḫu* = `סֻבִּין`; *nēbeḫu* = `אַבְנֵט` (used); *kapādu* = `חָשַׁב`; *tappûtu* = `חֲבֵרוּת`; *ištartu* = `אֵלָה` (vocalized `הָאֵלוֹת`); *qīštu* = `תְּשׁוּרָה`; *apu* = `אֲגַם`; *ḫaṣābu* = `גָּדַע`; *šalālu* = `שָׁלַל`, *šallu* = `שָׁלוּל`; *zērūtu* = `שִׂנְאָה`; *kulīlu* = `שַׁפִּירִית`; *neqelpû* = `צָף`; *ullânumma* = `מִיָּד`.
4. **Formula (§2):** `X – כשמעו את זאת,` confirmed (X 92, 163); *mindē-ma* = `אולי` (open decision 9).
5. **verify.py:** add `ṭīdiš` to the allowed lemmas of the `טיט` check (X 68–69, 145–146, 245–246 are the fixed passage's *ṭiṭṭiš emû*).

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A fixed passage written vocalized in the renderings table must be given in plene spelling too**, or each translator converts it differently (`זִיוְךָ` → "זיוך" or "זיווך"); the table should carry the plene string that the `>` lines must match, and the check should compare consonants after the strip test.
- **eBL's `Parallels:` field in a threefold refrain pairs lines with their negated or neighbouring counterparts** ("=" between X 40 and X 47, X 145 and X 69): compare the normalizations before treating a listed pair as identical, and justify every reported difference in the tablet's "Lines shared".
- **A reserved-word check keyed to lemmas must list every lemma form of the word** (here *ṭīdiš*, the adverb of *ṭīdu*), or the fixed passage itself fails the check.
- **Generating the annotated and clean files from one data file** (the eBL normalization inserted by script) removed every transcription and synchronization error for a 325-line tablet.

## Tablet II

### Scope
SB Tablet II, all 187 eBL labels in eBL's order (1, 26–51, 52-53, 56–66, a+1′–a+4′, 99–115, 162–202, 212–217, 218a, 219a, 218b, 219b, 220–243, 247–254, 260–302, 322): Enkidu before Šamḫat (1, the catch-line of I), the clothing and the shepherds' camp (26–43), bread and beer (44–58), Enkidu the herdsman and the man going to the wedding (59–a+4′), Enkidu in Uruk and the fight at the door of the wedding house (99–115), Ninsun on Enkidu and Enkidu's tears (162–192), Gilgameš's proposal (193–215), the guardian of the forest (216–229), "as for man, his days are numbered" (230–243), the casting of the weapons (247–254), the speech to the young men (260–271), Enkidu and the elders' counsel (272–302). Thirteen manuscripts (NinNA1a–b, BabNB1–4, BabaNB1, UrkNB1–3, three catch-line quotations); badly broken: about half of the tablet is lost (2–25, 54–55, 67–98, 116–161, 203–211, 244–246, 255–259, 303–321), and most surviving lines are partly restored from OB II (1–115) and OB III (165–302).

### Sources consulted
- eBL `L/1/4` SB II (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-SB-II/`): `reading-edition.md` (N, T, L, notes, parallels, ATF of every manuscript), `glossary.md` (stems), `manuscripts.md`.
- The first copies: OB II (`../2-הנוסח-הבבלי-הקדום/annotated-OB-II.rtl.md`, 45–226) and SB I (206, 212, 249–253, 267–268); `../parallels_for.py II --all` (133 lines with parallels). OB III (Yale) through eBL's normalizations quoted in `parallels_for.py` (not yet translated).
- `../renderings.md` incl. §3.1 (the fixed passage, first copies here), §9–§13; the format models; the flood corpus (XI 6 *aḫu* = `זרוע`).
- George's translation (eBL T), after drafting; Tchernichovsky 1924 (`../../sources/Ben-Yehuda--Tchernichovsky-1924/full-work--all-tablets.rtl.md`, his Tablets II–III) after pass (c).
- The vocalized Bible through `../../tools/check_bible_quotes.py --show`.

### Decisions
1. **Generated from one data file** (scratchpad `II-*.data.md` + `gen_II.py`): the eBL normalization is inserted by script; for a+1′–a+4′, whose N field is empty in `reading-edition.md`, the normalization printed under line 66 is used.
2. **OB II is the first copy and binds where the Akkadian is identical** (`../renderings.md` §13): 37 = OB II 75–76 verbatim (`אל סוכת הרועה, מקום הרבץ.`); 44 = OB II 87; 60 = OB II 115–116 (brackets only); 102 = OB II 202–203 (brackets only). **Where the SB wording changes, the Hebrew changes exactly as much**: 36 *ṣabtassū-ma* `אוחזת בו, וכְּאֵל` (OB *ṣabtat qāssu* `אוחזת בידו, כְּאֵל`); 38 D stative `מקובצים` (OB G preterite `נקבצו`); 40 *kī mašil lāna* `מה משול … בגוף`; 41 *šīḫu* `גבוה` (OB *šapil* `נמוך` – the SB makes Enkidu taller); 42 stative `אולי נולד בהר`; 46 *dagālu* `צופה` (OB *naṭālu u palāsu*); 61 stative `שוכבים`; **62 *nāqissunu* `נוֹקְדָם` (OB *maṣṣaršunu* `נוֹצְרָם`)**; **110 *pūḫu* `*תחליף*` (OB *meḫrum* `שווה`)**; 111 `בשער בית החתונה … ברגליו`; 112 present `נותן`; 113–115 (`נאחזו (זה בזה) בשער בית החתונה`, *gerû* Gt `התגרו (זה בזה)`, *râbu* / *nâšu* `רעדו … נע` for OB *abātu* / *ratātu* `הרסו … זע`).
3. **SB I binds its formulas and the dream lines**: 29 = I 206, 32 = I 212 (`יודע לבו –`), 43, 163 = I 268 (`כגוש של אָנוּ עזים כוחותיו`, with `אָנוּ` vocalized per §12), 103–107 = I 249–253, 162 = I 267; brackets and quotation marks follow this tablet's manuscripts and speeches.
4. **47–48, the key lines**: translated as eBL restores them – the SB **inverts the OB verbs** (bread: *lā lummud* `[לא לֻמַּד]`; beer: *ul īde* `[לא] ידע`). Only *-ma* (47) and *īde* (48) survive; the inversion rests on George's restoration plus the surviving *īde*, and this is said in the apparatus, the footnote and the index.
5. **The fixed passage §3.1 is copied from the table, in plene spelling without niqqud** (as the coordinator decided for §3.2): `הדרך הזאת – לא ללכת בה,` / `האדם ההוא – לא לראותו.` / `נוצר יער הארזים – עז הוא, לא ינום,` / `חומבבה – קולו מבול,` / `פיו – אש, ונפשו – מוות.` / `שומע הוא לשישים בֵּרוּ את המיית היער` (*qištīšu*: `יערו`; no final punctuation, as the table) / `מי זה ירד אל יערו?` / `אֲדַד – ראשון, והוא – שני.` / `מי זה יתגרה בו בָּאִיגִיגִי?` / `למען שלום הארזים` (no final punctuation) / `לאימות לאנשים גזר לו אֶנְלִיל,` / `והיורד אל יערו – תאחזהו רפיון.` Names are vocalized at their first occurrence only (Adad, Igigi, Enlil); `בֵּרוּ` is vocalized at every occurrence (ambiguous). **Adaptations by copy:** brackets per the ATF of each copy; 223 *qišti* `היער`, 280/293 *qištīšu* `יערו`; 294 has no *u* (`היורד`, not `והיורד`); 291–299 follow the Akkadian order of the elders' copy; 229, 286 end Enkidu's speeches (`רפיון."`) and 299 the elders' (`אנליל."`: a full stop and closing quote for the table's comma – marks only); 278–279 translate eBL's main reconstruction (UrkNB2), the variant couplet of BabNB4 / UrkNB3 (*aššu šullumi erēni* / *ana pulḫāti …*) is given in the apparatus.
6. ***naṣāru*** = `נוצר` (220, 277; never `שומר`); ***sakāpu*** `לא ינום` (Ps 121:4); ***napīšu*** (eBL's lemma in 222/279/292) = `נפשו`, as the table says (Job 41:13), although §9 gives *napīšu* = `נְשִׁימָה` for I 180, 188 (open decision 3).
7. ***manû*** "counted" (234) = `מנויים` [cognate of `לִמְנוֹת`, Ps 90:12]; ***šāru*** (235) = `רוח` (restored in most of the line: `[רוח]`); ***amēlūtu*** = `האנושות` with `ימיה` for *-ša*; the 3 m. sg. *īteppušu* rendered by the impersonal `עשה`; the possible Gtn not rendered (noted). `הבל` not used (reserved).
8. ***namṣaru*** (250) = `חרב`; ***ḫaṣṣinnu*** `גרזן`, ***pāšu*** `קרדום`, ***biltu*** (talent) `משא` + `(כל אחד)` for the distributive AM₃ (not `כִּכָּר`, which is *rebītu*, 114); ***patāqu*** (248) = `נְעַצֵּב` (creation corpus; vocalized, unvocalized it reads "sad").
9. ***karābu*** (265) = `בָּרְכוּנִי` (vocalized); ***saḫāru*** G (268 *lussaḫram*, checked: G precative, not D) = `אחזרה`, never `שוב` (↔ Gen 3:19, 28:21 – tagged as divergence); ***šulmu*** `בשלום`; ***akītu*** = `אָקִיתוּ` (transliterated, vocalized at first occurrence, note); ***nigûtu*** = `רינה`; ***raṣānu*** = `יהמו` (I 227 `הומים התופים`); ***ḫūd libbi*** = `בְּשִׂמְחַת לב` (vocalized: the string `שמחת` is Šamḫat's name trap, verify.py).
10. ***ṭēma turru*** (288) = `טעם השיבו` – the exact Hebrew idiom `מְשִׁיבֵי טָעַם` (Prov 26:16) – although *ṭēmu* is `דעת` in 31, 39 and I 8 (open decision 4). ***libbu našû*** (289) = `לבך נשאך` (Exod 35:21 `נְשָׂאוֹ לִבּוֹ`). ***ṣeḫru*** (289) = `צעיר` (cognate; VA+BM has `קטן` for the child; open decision 5). ***awû*** Gt (290) = `שַׂחְתָּ`.
11. ***ḫarrānu***: `הדרך` in the fixed passage (as the §3.1 table), `אוֹרַח` (vocalized: "guest") in 264, per §5 – an inconsistency inherited from the table (open decision 1).
12. Other new renderings (all grepped against every earlier `>` line): *nanzih* (ezēḫu N) `הֵחָגֵר`; *mitluku* `נמלך עם עצמו`; *ina ṭēmīšu-ma* `מדעתו שלו`; *naburriš* `כצריח` (not `מגדל`: Gen 11:4); *iptēq* `צמצם (את עיניו)` (OB); *dagālu* `צופה`; *bêšu* `זז` (OB II 6); *pūḫu* `*תחליף*` (not `תמורה`, the root of *nakāru* D `הֵמִיר`); *râbu* `רעד` (as VI); *nâšu* G `נע` (beside D `הֵנִיעַ`, open decision 2); *ṣarpiš* `במרירות`; *unamba* (*nabû* "wail", eBL's lemma "to name" is a slip) `מְיַלֵּל`; *kimtu u salātu* `משפחה ושאר`; *uššurtu* (*wašāru* G stat.) `משתלשלות`; *uštaddan* `נדבר (עם עצמו)`; *aḫu* "arm" `זרוע` (flood XI 6), *ramû* III G `רפה`; *mitḫāriš* `בשווה`; *ṣarāpu* Dt `נִצְרַף` (Ps 26:2; `צרב` is *qamû*, X 44); *dimmatu* `אנקה`; *tuša* `כאילו`; *ekēpu* Gt `ממשמש ובא`; *umāšu* `*היאחזות*` (not `נאבק`, Gen 32:25); *idu* "side" `לצדך`; *nakāsu* `כרת`; *šebēru* `שבר`; *ašamšūtu* `עלעולים` (`סופה` = *mehû*, `סער` = *imḫullu*, *ūmu*); *pisnuqiš* `כחלש`; *ramû* III + *pû* `ופיך רפה` (eBL lemma *ramāmu* rejected); *lemēnu* D `הֵרַעְתָּ`; *abātu* II `נסו` (≠ *abātu* I `הרס`); *šaḫāṭu* `זינקו` (VI); *kiškattû* `כּוּר` (vocalized; `כור` = *kurru*); *mâḫu* `[אצא?]`; *gapāšu* `נועזתי` (Isa 33:19 `עַם נוֹעָז`; avoids `עז` = *dannu*, `אמיץ` = *qardu*); *maḫāru* G "confront" `אפגוש` (Gt/Št `נפגשו`/`פגש פנים אל פנים`); *rakābu* `ארכב`; *misarru* `מזח` (VII); *milka malāku* `עצה יעץ` (*māliku* `יועץ`).
13. **Quotation marks**: speeches in `"…"`; openings at the first surviving line of a speech whose start is lost (28, 50, 162, 168), as in VII.
14. **eBL slips recorded**: *ṣēru* lemmatized "back" (29, 177, 237); 40 *eṭlu* as adjective; 174 *unamba* as *nabû* "to name"; 233 *irmâm* as *ramāmu*; 273 *eṭlūti* as *eṭlūtu* "manliness"; 107's parallel I 281 / I 280 (wrong lines); 226's parallel II 282 (should be 283); 254, 265, 269, 282–289 linked to I 254–291 (`../renderings.md` §9: false); 193 *pa-a-ša₂* (the edition's own emendation `pâšu!`).

### Open decisions for the user
1. **§3.1 *ḫarrānu šī* = `הַדֶּרֶךְ הַזֹּאת`** (kept, as the table) against §5 *ḫarrānu* = `אֹרַח` (264 here; X's `באורח רחוקה`): change the fixed passage to `הָאֹרַח הַזֹּאת – לֹא לָלֶכֶת בָּהּ` before III and V copy it?
2. **115 *nâšu* G = `הקיר נע`** (chosen: consistent with *nâšu* D = `הֵנִיעַ`, and the real parallel is Isa 6:4 `וַיָּנֻעוּ אַמּוֹת הַסִּפִּים`), or avoid the string `נע` of Gen 4:12 `נָע וָנָד` (alternatives: `זע`, the OB *ratātu*; `התנודד`, which has the root of `נָד` and is worse)?
3. **222/279/292 *napīšu* = `נפשו`** (the §3.1 table, ↔ Job 41:13 `נַפְשׁוֹ גֶּחָלִים תְּלַהֵט`) against §9 *napīšu* = `נְשִׁימָה` (I 180, 188): keep the fixed passage as is (chosen), or `ונשימתו – מוות` everywhere?
4. **288 *ṭēma turru* = `טעם השיבו`** (Prov 26:16 `מְשִׁיבֵי טָעַם`, chosen) or `דעת השיבו` (*ṭēmu* = `דעת` elsewhere)?
5. **289 *ṣeḫru* = `צעיר`** (cognate, chosen) against OB VA+BM's `קָטָן`: make `צעיר` the corpus rule for "young" and keep `קטן` for "small/child"?
6. **110 *pūḫu* = `*תחליף*`** (chosen) or `תְּמוּרָה` (Lev 27:10; root of *nakāru* D `הֵמִיר`)?
7. **262 *agdapuš* = `נועזתי`** (chosen) or `גבה לבי` (2 Chr 17:6; but `גבה` is *šīḫu* / *šaqû* D)? III 24 will copy it.

- 2026-10-17: read the brief, the whole guidelines, corpus.md, renderings.md (incl. §9–§13), both project files with their tablet sections, the format models; ran `parallels_for.py II --all` (133 lines); read the whole of eBL SB II (N, T, L, notes, ATF) and the OB II Hebrew of the parallel lines.
- Drafted all 187 entries in 16 sections into three data files and generated `annotated-II.rtl.md` and `translation-II.rtl.md` (15 footnotes).
- Pass (a) fidelity: every `>` line re-read against N and the ATF. Changes: 107 brackets moved to the verb (`u₂-n[a…`, only one witness); 103 `[עליו]` (UGU broken in both witnesses, unlike I 249); 41/164 `צריח` and `[מפואר?]`; 47 bracketed whole (no ATF witness); 267 vocalized `בְּשִׂמְחַת` (verify.py's Šamḫat trap); 34–35 chain `לבוש אחד … ולבוש שני` (the SB order, against the OB); 239 variants recorded.
- Pass (b) consistency: speech formulas (165–167, 188, 193, 216, 230), the *kiṣru* and *dān emūqī* formulas, the §3.1 passage character for character in its three copies, names vocalized at first occurrence (`אֶנְקִידוּ`, `גִּלְגָּמֶשׁ`, `שַׁמְחַת` always, `אוּרוּךְ`, `אִשְׁחַרָה`, `רִימַת־נִינְסוּן`, `חוּמְבָּבָה`, `אֶנְלִיל`, `אֲדַד`, `אִיגִיגִי`, `אָנוּ` always, `רֵעִי` always); reserved strings grepped in the plain `>` lines (`ידע` 10× only for *edû*/*mūdû*; `שׂים` only *šakānu*; `שב` only in `ישב`/`ישברו`/`שבעה`; no `לקח`, `שוב`, `טיט`, `עפר`, `עשב`, `קרא`, `שמר`, `הבל`, `נד`; `עץ` only inside `יעץ`); every new string grepped against all earlier `>` lines (215 changed from `הסופות` to `העלעולים`; 241 `כּוּר` vocalized against *kurru*). Quote fixes: 38 (1 Sam 22:2), 233 (1 Sam 1:8 instead of Gen 21:11), 263.
- Pass (c) Hebrew: read aloud; kept the literal `[אל] אנקידו דבר מדבר`, `אוֹרַח … ארכב` and `לגילגמש לבוא לא נותן`. Tchernichovsky 1924 compared (his II–III): he has `שומר הפקיד אנליל לשמר את הארזים` (`שמר`, Gen 3:24), `ארזי־הקדש`, `חרשת־הקדש`, `יער האלים`, `בן־שחץ` (Job 41:26), `לבו ינוד`, `חית־השדה`, and bread **and wine** (`הגישה לפניו מן הלחם והיין`) – none of these in the Akkadian; nothing changed in ours.
- `python3 -I verify.py II`: 187 entries = eBL 187, 187 translation lines, problems 0 (59 differences in repeated lines and 31 in marks only, each justified below). `check_bible_quotes.py` on both files and this section: 0 problems.

| 234 | amēlūtu manû ūmūša | האנושות – מנויים ימיה | `לִמְנוֹת יָמֵינוּ כֵּן הוֹדַע` (תהלים צ:יב) | [cognate] | **Key.** *manû* ~ מנה and *ūmu* ~ יום: the same two roots. = OB III 142. |
| 234 | amēlūtu manû ūmūša | האנושות – מנויים ימיה | `וְהָיוּ יָמָיו מֵאָה וְעֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה` (בראשית ו:ג) | [equivalent] | **Key.** The limit of man's days; in Genesis a divine decree, here a hero's maxim (OB III: after "only the gods dwell forever with Šamaš"). |

| 234 | manû ūmūša | מנויים ימיה | `הוֹדִיעֵנִי יְהוָה קִצִּי וּמִדַּת יָמַי מַה-הִיא` (תהלים לט:ה) | [equivalent] | The measure of one's days. |

| 235 | šārum-ma | [רוח] | `זְכֹר כִּי-רוּחַ חַיָּי` (איוב ז:ז) | [equivalent] | Life is wind. |
| 237 | taʾʾaldam-ma tarbâ ina ṣēri | נולדת וגדלת בשדה | `וַיִּגְדָּל; וַיֵּשֶׁב בַּמִּדְבָּר` (בראשית כא:כ) | [equivalent] | The child of the wild (Ishmael). |
| 238 | išḫuṭūkā-ma labbū | זינקו עליך לבאים | `וְהִנֵּה כְּפִיר אֲרָיוֹת שֹׁאֵג לִקְרָאתוֹ` (שופטים יד:ה) | [equivalent] | The hero attacked by a lion. |

| 241 | ana kiškattî | אל הכּוּר | `וַיּוֹצִא אֶתְכֶם מִכּוּר הַבַּרְזֶל` (דברים ד:כ) | [equivalent] | The furnace of the smiths. |
| 248–250 | ḫaṣṣinnī niptiqu … pāšī … namṣārīšunu | גרזנים נְעַצֵּב … קרדומים … חרבותיהם | `תּוּבַל קַיִן לֹטֵשׁ כָּל חֹרֵשׁ נְחֹשֶׁת וּבַרְזֶל` (בראשית ד:כב) | [equivalent] | Metal weapons cast before the expedition. *patāqu* = עצב (the creation verb). |
| 250 | namṣārīšunu | חרבותיהם | `וְאֵת לַהַט הַחֶרֶב הַמִּתְהַפֶּכֶת לִשְׁמֹר אֶת דֶּרֶךְ עֵץ הַחַיִּים` (בראשית ג:כד) | [divergence] | **Key.** *namṣaru* = חרב [equivalent]: in Genesis the sword guards the way, here the swords are made against the guardian. |

| 265 | kurbāʾinnī-ma lullik | בָּרְכוּנִי, ואלכה | `לֹא אֲשַׁלֵּחֲךָ כִּי אִם בֵּרַכְתָּנִי` (בראשית לב:כז) | [equivalent] | Blessing before going on. |

| 266 | pānīkunu lūmur | את פניכם אראה | `כִּי עַל כֵּן רָאִיתִי פָנֶיךָ כִּרְאֹת פְּנֵי אֱלֹהִים` (בראשית לג:י) | [equivalent] | Seeing the faces again after the journey; restored. |

| 268 | lussaḫram-ma | אחזרה | `וְשַׁבְתִּי בְשָׁלוֹם אֶל בֵּית אָבִי` (בראשית כח:כא) | [divergence] | *saḫāru* = חזר, not *târu* (שוב, reserved for `עַד שׁוּבְךָ`, ג:יט): the return is not rendered with `שוב`. |
| 268–270 | akīta ina šatti šinīšu lūpuš | ואת האָקִיתוּ בשנה [פעמיים אעשה] | `שָׁלֹשׁ רְגָלִים תָּחֹג לִי בַּשָּׁנָה` (שמות כג:יד) | [equivalent] | The yearly festivals; the *akītu* was kept twice a year (spring and autumn). |
| 268 | akīta ina šatti šinīšu | האקיתו בשנה פעמיים | `וְחַג הָאָסִיף תְּקוּפַת הַשָּׁנָה` (שמות לד:כב) | [equivalent] | The turn of the year. |

| 274 | lā illak ana qišti erēni | ולא ילך אל יער הארזים | `וּמֵעֵץ הַדַּעַת טוֹב וָרָע לֹא תֹאכַל מִמֶּנּוּ` (בראשית ב:יז) | [divergence] | A prohibition to approach the forbidden place – from a friend and the elders, not from the god. |

| 289 | ṣeḫrēta gilgāmeš | צעיר אתה, גילגמש | `כִּי נַעַר אַתָּה וְהוּא אִישׁ מִלְחָמָה מִנְּעֻרָיו` (שמואל א יז:לג) | [equivalent] | **Key.** The young hero warned before facing the giant; *ṣeḫru* ~ צעיר [cognate]. |

### Lines shared with other tablets
- **OB II (first copy)**: identical where the Akkadian is: 37 = OB II 75–76; 44 = 87; 60 = 115–116 and 102 = 202–203 (brackets only). Changed exactly where the SB changes (decision 2; `verify.py` lists them): 1 ~ 45; 29 ~ 54–55; 34 ~ 69–70; 35 ~ 71–72; 36 ~ 73–74; 38 ~ 77; 40 ~ 80, 183; 41 ~ 81–82, 184–185; 42 ~ 83–84, 186–187; 46 ~ 88–89; 47–48 ~ 90–93 (verbs inverted); 49 ~ 94–95 (the *ana šâšū-ma* formula for OB's *pîša īpušam-ma*); 50–51 ~ 96–98; 61 ~ 117; 62 ~ 118–119; 63 ~ 120–121; 100 ~ 179–180, 200–201; 103 ~ 10; 107 ~ 11, 21; 109 ~ 196–197; 110 ~ 194–195; 111 ~ 215–216; 112 ~ 217; 113 ~ 218–219; 114 ~ 214; 115 ~ 220–221, 225–226. eBL's links OB II 33–34 ~ 254 and OB II 43 ~ 283 are false.
- **SB I**: 29 = I 206 (brackets, quote mark); 32 ~ I 212 (*-ma*, brackets); 43 = I 268 (`אָנוּ` vocalized per §12, closing quote); 163 = I 268 (brackets); 103–106 = I 249–252 / 277–280, 107 = I 253 (brackets only); 162 = I 267 (brackets, opening quote). eBL's links of II 254, 265, 269, 282–289 to I 254–291 and of 107 to I 280–281 are false (`../renderings.md` §9).
- **Within the tablet**: 32 ~ 59 ~ 240 (*mūdû libbu*); 41 = 164; 43 = 163; 180 ~ 186, 181 ~ 187; 261 ~ 273; the fixed passage 218a–229 = 274–286 = 291–299 (decision 5; differences: brackets; 223 *qišti* vs 280/293 *qištīšu*; 294 without *u*; 229/286/299 closing quotes; 278–279 variant couplet; eBL's 226 ~ 282 is a slip for 283).
- **First copies for later tablets** (III, V, OB III to copy): the §3.1 passage (218a–229); 232–233 (= V 130–131); 262–271 (= III 24–34, 48–50: `נועזתי – אלך דרך רחוקה, אל מקום חומבבה,` / `קרב אשר לא ידעתי אפגוש,` / `אוֹרַח אשר לא ידעתי ארכב.` / `בָּרְכוּנִי, ואלכה אני,` / `את פניכם אראה בשלום,` / `אבואה בשער אורוך בְּשִׂמְחַת לב,` / `אחזרה, ואת האָקִיתוּ בשנה פעמיים אעשה,` / `אעשה את האקיתו בשנה פעמיים.` / `האקיתו תוּשַׂם, ורינה תהיה,` / `התופים יהמו לפני רימת־נינסון!` – III changes *ḫarrāna* to *gerra*, 3rd person in 49–50, and *ina maḫrīki*); 289 (~ V 167 *ṣehrēta gilgāmeš*); the speech formulas of 165–167, 193, 216; OB III 108–112, 129–143, 151–163, 180, 189–202 (the Yale tablet's versions of these lines).

### Proposals for the shared renderings
1. **§3.1**: record the plene forms of the first copy (decision 5) as the binding strings, with "no final punctuation" for *išemmē-ma …* and *aššu šullumi erēni*; add the note that eBL's lemma in *napīssu* is *napīšu* (open decision 3); and decide *ḫarrānu šī* (open decision 1) before III.
2. **Key terms (§4)**: *pūḫu* = `*תחליף*` (beside *meḫru* = `שווה`; open decision 6); *manû* (adj.) = `מָנוּי` [cognate ↔ Ps 90:12]; *šāru* = `רוּחַ` (never `הֶבֶל`); *namṣaru* = `חֶרֶב` (used); *ṭēma turru* = `הֵשִׁיב טַעַם` (open decision 4); *libbu našû* = `נְשָׂאוֹ לִבּוֹ`; *ṣeḫru* "young" = `צָעִיר` (open decision 5); *akītu* = `אָקִיתוּ`; *nāqidu* + suffix `נוֹקְדָם`.
3. **Ordinary words (§5)**: *ezēḫu* N = `הֵחָגֵר`; *naburriš* = `כְּצָרִיחַ`; *dagālu* = `צָפָה` (used); *bêšu* = `זָז`; *râbu* = `רָעַד`; *nâšu* G = `נָע` (open decision 2); *ṣarpiš* = `בִּמְרִירוּת`; *kimtu u salātu* = `מִשְׁפָּחָה וּשְׁאֵר`; *wašāru* G stat. = `מִשְׁתַּלְשֵׁל`; *ramû* III G = `רָפָה`; *mitḫāriš* = `בְּשָׁוֶה`; *ṣarāpu* = `צָרַף`; *dimmatu* = `אֲנָקָה`; *tuša* = `כְּאִלּוּ`; *ekēpu* Gt = `מְמַשְׁמֵשׁ וּבָא`; *umāšu* = `*הֵיאָחֲזוּת*`; *ašamšūtu* = `עַלְעוֹל`; *pisnuqiš* = `כְּחַלָּשׁ`; *lemēnu* D = `הֵרַע`; *abātu* II = `נָס`; *kiškattû* = `כּוּר` (always vocalized); *mâḫu* = `יָצָא` (?); *gapāšu* = `נוֹעַז` (open decision 7); *maḫāru* G "confront" = `פָּגַשׁ`; *nakāsu* = `כָּרַת`; *šebēru* = `שָׁבַר`; *nigûtu* = `רִנָּה`; *raṣānu* = `הָמָה` (used); *milka malāku* = `יָעַץ עֵצָה`; *biltu* (weight) = `מַשָּׂא` + `(כל אחד)` for AM₃; *ḫūd libbi* = `שִׂמְחַת לֵב` (always vocalized `שִׂמְחַת`: verify.py's Šamḫat check).
4. **§7 "always vocalize"**: add `כּוּר` (furnace / *kurru* measure), `אוֹרַח` (road / guest), `בָּרְכוּנִי`, `נְעַצֵּב` (cast / sad), `שִׂמְחַת` (Šamḫat), `הֵחָגֵר`, `בֵּרוּ` (every occurrence).
5. **verify.py**: no change needed. A possible check: the §3.1 strings (as for §3.2 in X) for III and V.

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **An OB "first copy" can be changed by the later version in exactly the key words**: SB II replaces OB II's *maṣṣaru* "keeper" with *nāqidu* "herdsman" (62), *meḫru* "equal" with *pūḫu* "substitute" (110), inverts the verbs of not-knowing and not-being-taught (47–48), and makes Enkidu taller instead of shorter (41). Copying the OB Hebrew line by line made each change visible; compare the OB and SB normalizations word by word before copying.
- **eBL's `Parallels:` field in a badly broken tablet links lines by position in the manuscript, not by content** (II 226 → 282, 107 → I 280–281, 254 → I 282, 278–279 → the variant couplet): check every "=" against the normalization before treating a difference as an error.
- **A name-trap check on unvocalized strings catches ordinary words too** (`בשמחת לב` read as Šamḫat): vocalize the common noun rather than avoid the idiom.

## Tablet III

### Scope
SB Tablet III, all 209 eBL labels in eBL's order (1–97, 97a, 98–122, 122a, 123–136, 146–158, 149′–164′, 164–173, 202–205, 207–233, 244): the end of the elders' counsel (1–12: "let Enkidu go before you … protect the friend, keep safe the comrade"), Gilgameš and Enkidu go to Egalmaḫ (13–22), Gilgameš asks Ninsun's blessing (23–34 ~ II 262–271), Ninsun's purification, dressing and ascent to the roof with incense (35–45), her prayer to Šamaš: the restless heart, Aya's reminder, the road, the long days and short nights, the thirteen winds, Šamaš's evening rest (46–99), her second plea ("will he not share heaven with you?", 100–115), the adoption of Enkidu among the temple personnel (116–128), her charge to Enkidu (129–135), fragments of the departure (146–173, 149′–164′ = NinNA2a col. v, George's separate numbering), Gilgameš's instructions to his officers ("judge the case of the weak", 202–211), the officers' homage (212–214), the elders' blessing repeated (215–227), Enkidu's first words to Gilgameš (228–233). Twelve manuscripts (NinNA1a–b, NinNA2a–d, BabNB1a–b, BabNB2a–b, BabNBSch1, UrkLB1); 1–135 well preserved (mostly two or three witnesses), 136–233 fragmentary, 137–145, 174–201, 206, 234–243 lost.

### Sources consulted
- eBL `L/1/4` SB III (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-SB-III/`): `reading-edition.md` (N, T, L, notes, ATF of every manuscript), `manuscripts.md`.
- `../parallels_for.py III --all` (91 lines with parallels); the first copies in SB II (262–271, the speech formulas), SB I (257–258, 272, 284–285, 31–32, 253), VI 34, VII 141–143, X 34–38, XI 301; OB III 215, 245–264 through eBL's normalizations (OB III not yet translated).
- `../renderings.md` incl. §9–§14 (binding); both `project.md` files with their tablet sections; the format models (flood XI 1–120, Enūma eliš VI 1–60); `annotated-II.rtl.md` as the nearest model.
- George's translation (eBL T), after drafting; Tchernichovsky 1924 (`../../sources/Ben-Yehuda--Tchernichovsky-1924/full-work--all-tablets.rtl.md`, ll. 1370–1460, his Tablet III) after pass (c).
- The vocalized Bible through `../../tools/check_bible_quotes.py`.

### Decisions
1. **Generated from one data file** (scratchpad `III-[abcd].data.md` + `gen_III.py`), as for II and X: the eBL normalization is inserted by script; for 122a and 149′–164′, whose N field is empty, a normalization is built from the ATF line printed under 158 (`[...] ...`, `[... | har]rāna`, `[... | gilgām]eš`, `[...] | gilgāmeš`).
2. **The elders' blessing, first copy fixed at 1–12, copied at 215–227**: identical Hebrew except brackets (each copy's own manuscripts), the vocalization of first occurrences, and two changes the Akkadian makes: 219 *liṣṣur* (precative) `[ינצור]` for 5 *iṣṣur* (preterite) `נצר`, and the extra line 223 (`[אל] מבואות ההרים […]`, cf. OB III 253) between 222 and 224. 216 *tattakkal* and 2 *tatakkil* differ in form, not in meaning: same Hebrew.
3. ***naṣāru* = `נָצַר`** (5, 9, 135, 219, 224), never `שָׁמַר`; ***maṣṣartu*** "watch (of the night)" (57, 75) = `נְצִירוֹת` (law corpus *maṣṣarūtum* = `נְצִירָה`), **not `אַשְׁמוּרוֹת`**, so that no שמר string enters and the *naṣāru* chain stays visible (the Gen 4:9 / 3:24 rows depend on it).
4. ***šalāmu* D (9, 224 *lišallim*) = `יביא בשלום`** – two words for one verb, keeping the root שלם (§3.1 `למען שלום הארזים`; 1/29/215 *šulmu* `בשלום`). Rejected: `ישלים` / `יְשַׁלֵּם` (read "complete" / "pay"; `שִׁלֵּם` is reserved for *šullumu* "pay" in the law corpus), `ישמור` (reserved), `יגן` (hides the root), `יציל` (taken by *ezēbu* Š in 4: the SB proverb replaced OB III 255 *ušallim* by *ušezzeb*, and the Hebrew shows the replacement). *wabālu* in 10 is therefore `ישא` (not `יביא`).
5. **New road words**, kept apart: *ṭūdu* = `נָתִיב` (5, 219), *gerru* = `מְסִלָּה` (27, 50, 76, 231), beside *urḫu* = `דרך`, *ḫarrānu* = `אוֹרַח` (always vocalized), *kibsu* = `שביל`. **27 changes II 264**: SB III has *gerra* where II has *ḫarrāna*, so `[מסי]לה אשר לא ידעתי אר[כב].` against II's `אוֹרַח [אשר לא ידעתי ארכב].`
6. **Ninsun's ritual**: *narmaku* = `מֶרְחָץ`; *elēlu* Dt + *ramānu* = `טיהרה את עצמה`; *bīnu* "tamarisk" = `אֶשֶׁל` (eBL's lemma "son" is a slip: {giš}ŠINIG); *tullal* ("you purify", soapwort) = `בּוֹרִית` (Jer 2:22), the name's meaning in the note; *raqqatu* `(אריג) דק`; *agû* / *apāru* = `כתר` / `חבושה` (VI 5); *simmiltu* `מדרגות` (I 15); *ūru* "roof" `גג`; ***qutrinnu* = `קְטוֹרֶת`** [cognate, *qatāru*] and *qutrinna šakānu* = `שׂמה קטורת` (Deut 33:10 `יָשִׂימוּ קְטוֹרָה`), not `הקטירה`; *surqinnu* = `מנחה` (flood XI 158); *idīša išši* `נשאה את זרועותיה` (not `פרשה כפיה`); *kabātu* D `הִכְבִּידָה`.
7. **Ninsun's prayer**: *libbu lā ṣālilu* = `לב אשר אינו ישן` (I 237 `אינו ישן`); *lapātu* `נגע`; *dapīnu* = `עָרִיץ`; *mimma lemnu* = `כל דבר רע`; *zêru* `שונא` (54 eBL lemma *ezēru* "curse" is a slip, 205 has *zêru*); *ḫalāqu* D `יְאַבֵּד` (vocalized); *itû* `גבול`; ***Aya* = `אַיָּה`, always vocalized** (plain `איה` = "where?"); ***kallatu* = `כַּלָּה`** [cognate] (see open decision 1); *ḫasāsu* `תזכירך`; *barārītu* `דמדומים`; *ṣītu* `מוצא` (I 40); *ruššû* `*אדמומית*`; *quʾʾû* = `קִוָּה` [cognate]; *puqqu* = `מייחל` (not `צפה` = *dagālu*); *dipāru* `לפיד` (flood XI 104); *nepelkû* Š `ירחיב`; *nasāqu* Št `יכשיר`; *arraku* / *kurrû* `ארוכים` / `קצרים`; *purīdu* `פסיעות`; *karāšu* `מחנה`; *nubattu* `מלון` (§2); *emēdu* N "meet in battle" = `נתקל בּ־` (not `נפגש` = *maḫāru* Gt, not `נסמך` = *emēdu* N "lean"); *dekû* `העֵר … על` (Isa 13:17); the thirteen winds: `רוח דרום, רוח צפון, רוח קדים, רוח מערב, נשיבה (*ziqqu*, as VI 34), נשיבה־סוערת` / `שַׁפַּרְזִיקּוּ, סַעַר (*imḫullu*, II), סִימוּרְרוּ, אַסַּכּוּ` / `קָרָה (*šuruppû*; `כפור` = *šarbu*), סוּפָה (*mehû*), עַלְעוֹל (*ašamšūtu*)`; *eṭû* `חשך` (Qal; *ekēlu* = Hiphil); *parû* `פרדה`; *pānī šakānu ana* = `שׂים פנים אל` (Gen 31:21); *ḫarāpu* Š `*יקדימוך*`; *šubtu nēḫtu*, *mayyāl mūši* as VII 142 / I 230; *nadû* (bed) `מושלך` (OB II 196); *nindabû* = `תרומות־לחם` (≠ *surqinnu* `מנחה`); *sissiktu* `כנף`; *ebbu* `צח`; ***kapāru* II "wipe" = `כִּפֵּר`** [cognate], vocalized (Gen 32:21 `אֲכַפְּרָה פָנָיו`).
8. **The second plea (100–115)**: *târu-ma* + verb "again" kept as `שבה, ו…` (*târu* = שוב); *šanû* D = `שׁוֹנָה` (vocalized) [cognate]; *uʾʾurtu* = `צַו`; *izzazza* = `יחלוק` (eBL's note: *zâzu* Gt), as George; *ḫaṭṭu* `שרביט`; *emēqu* `יחכם`; *bêlu* `ימשול`; Irnini `אִירְנִינִי`, Kurnugi `כּוּרְנוּגִי` (transliterated, vocalized, "the land of no return" in the note), Apsû `האַפְּסוּ`, Sîn `סִין`, Ea `אֵאָה`, Ningišzida `נִינְגִּשְׁזִידָה` (§1); *banû* (107, uncertain) `אֶבְנֵהוּ?`; *ilūtu* `אֵלוּת`; *erēbu* Š `מביא` (Hiphil of the *erēbu* verb `בא`).
9. **The adoption (116–128)**: *ṭēma šakānu* = `שׂמה טַעַם` (the Biblical Aramaic idiom `שִׂים טְעֵם`, Ezra 4:19; `טַעַם` vocalized), *šasû* `צעקה אל`; *ṣīt ūrīya* = `מוֹצָא ערוותי` (*ūru* = `ערוה`, §9; *ṣītu* = `מוצא`); *watmu* `אֶפְרוֹחִים`; *šerku* = `נְתִינִים` [equivalent, Ezra 2:58]; *ugbabtu* = `כּוֹהֶנֶת־אוּגְבַּבְתּוּ`, *qadištu* = `קְדֵשָׁה` [cognate], *kulmašītu* = `כוּלְמַשִּׁית` (the §11 pattern for cultic titles); *indī* `*סימנים*` (George's italics; eBL lemma *imdu*); *tikku* `עורף` (VI); ***leqû* … *liqûtu* = `לקחו את הנלקח`** (figura etymologica; not `לָקוּחַ`, modern "customer"); ***rabû* D … *tarbûtu* = `מגדלות את המגודל`** (the cognate `תַּרְבּוּת`, Num 32:14, only in the apparatus: it reads "culture"); *mārāt ilī* = `בנות האלים`; ***leqû ana mārūti* = `לקחתי לבן`** (Esth 2:7); *damāqu* D = `יעשה עמו חסד` (the verb of *dumqu* = `חסד`, §10; avoids `ייטיב` and the `טוב` family).
10. **The departure**: *dīn enši dīn* = `דִּין חַלָּשׁ – דִּין!` (the Hebrew idiom, Jer 22:16, Prov 31:9; *dīnu* = `דין`, law corpus; `דִּין` imperative vocalized); *buʾʾû* `חַפֵּשׂ`; *šerru laʾû* `תינוק פעוט` (I 253, II 107); *nizmatu* `מאוויים`; *zaqāpu* `זקף`; *šakkanakku* `מושל`; *lasāmu* Gtn = `מתרוצצים` (no `שוב ושוב`, §11); *paḫāru* D (208) `לא יקבצו` (not the Gen 41 context); *rašû* `קנה`; *saḫāru* D (230) `הַחֲזֵר` (not `השיב` = *târu* D); *burāšu* = `ברוש` [cognate]; *šaḫtu* `הנכנע`; *kutallu* `אחורי`; *irtu* (of water) `חזה` (§12), kept literally; 173 the journey formula in the imperative, `[פּוֹת פַּת?]`.
11. **eBL slips recorded**: 36 *ištenemme* lemmatized *šeʾû* "seek" (it is *šemû* Gtn); 38 *bīni* as "son"; 54 *tazerru* as *ezēru* "curse" (205 *zêru*); 133 *qablāka* as *qablu* II "battle" (83 has *qablu* I "hips"); 208, 213 *eṭlūtu* as "manliness" (plural of *eṭlu*, as II 273); 216 *tattakkal* as *akālu* "eat"; 88's parallel "V 137" (the execution is V 159–163).
12. **What the brief asked to watch for and is not in SB III's surviving text**: *šuma šakānu* "to establish a name" (Gen 11:4, 6:4) and "the eternal name" are in OB III 148, 188 (*šumī lū ušzīz*, *šuma ša darû … luštaknam*), not in SB III (its counterpart, 137–145 / 174–201, is lost); the §3.1 guardian passage does not recur (231 *gerra lā …* may open a similar warning, but is broken, so §3.1 is not copied). No parallels row is invented for them (one [absent] row records it).

### Open decisions for the user
1. ***kallatu* (56, 74, 86, 99, Aya "the Bride") and *ḫīrtu* (10, 225, "wives")** now both read `כַּלָּה` / `כלות`: *kallatu* is the true cognate of `כַּלָּה` (chosen, guidelines §6.4 rule 2), and §4 gives *ḫīrtu* = `כַּלָּה` (kept, binding). Proposal: move *ḫīrtu* to another word corpus-wide (`בְּחִירָה`, which shows *ḫâru* "choose" ↔ Gen 6:2 `מִכֹּל אֲשֶׁר בָּחָרוּ`; or `רַעְיָה`, but that is *marḫītu*), and keep `כַּלָּה` for *kallatu*.
2. ***šalāmu* D = `יביא בשלום`** (9, 224) or another rendering (decision 4)? The same verb in OB III 255 (*tappâm ušallim*) will need the same Hebrew.
3. ***maṣṣartu* = `נְצִירוֹת`** (57, 75), or the natural `אַשְׁמוּרוֹת` with a note (it would bring the root שמר into the `>` lines)?
4. ***qutrinnu* = `קְטוֹרֶת`** (cognate, chosen) – and should *qutrinna šakānu* stay `שׂמה קטורת` (*šakānu* = `שׂים`, with Deut 33:10 `יָשִׂימוּ קְטוֹרָה`) rather than the smoother `הקטירה`?
5. ***kapāru* II "wipe" = `כִּפֵּר`** (99; cognate, Gen 32:21 `אֲכַפְּרָה פָנָיו`) – keep the cognate (a reader may hear "atone"), or `ינגב` with the cognate in the note?
6. ***gerru* = `מְסִלָּה`** (new; Isa 40:3 `מְסִלָּה`, Num 20:19): confirm for the corpus (IV, V will have it); and *ṭūdu* = `נָתִיב`.
7. ***šerku* = `נְתִינִים`** (122; [equivalent], Ezra 2:58) – a biblical technical term for temple servants; acceptable, or a neutral `מוקדשים`?
8. ***liqûtu* / *tarbûtu* = `הנלקח` / `המגודל`** (125–126), keeping the figura etymologica, against the cognate `תַּרְבּוּת` (Num 32:14), which reads "culture" today.

Strip test: `קְטֹרֶת` > `קְטוֹרֶת`, `בֹּרִית` > `בּוֹרִית`, `אֶפְרֹחֶיךָ` > `אֶפְרוֹחֶיךָ`, `כֻלְמַשִּׁיּוֹת` > `כוּלְמַשִּׁיּוֹת`, `הַמְגֻדָּל` > `הַמְגוּדָּל`, `פֹּת` > `פּוֹת`, `הָאֲנוּנ[נָּכִּי` corrected. ### Lines shared with other tablets
- **Within the tablet**: 1–12 = 215–227 (first copy 1–12; 219 precative `[ינצור]` for 5 `נצר`; 223 added; brackets per copy; 216 vs 2 different verb forms, same Hebrew); 6 ~ 77 ~ 220; 7 ~ 78 ~ 221; 25–27 ~ 48–50 (1st / 3rd person); 51–54 ~ 202–205 (3rd sg. / 1st pl.; 54 "you hate" / 205 "Šamaš hates"); 52 ~ 81 (with the name) ~ 131 (*alāku*); 56 = 74 = 86 (brackets; final `:` / `!`); 57 = 75; 82–84 ~ 132–134 (3rd / 2nd person, precative / imperative; final marks); 93 ~ 164 (singular / plural weapons); 95 ~ 165 (2nd / 3rd person, restored); 17 ~ 117 (Ninsun / Rīmat-Ninsun); 15 ~ 20–21; 149 ~ 170.
- **SB II (first copy)**: 24–34 ~ II 262–271: 24–25 split II 262 (`נועזתי – [אלך]` / `דרך רחוקה, אל מקום חו[מבבה],`); 26 = II 263 (brackets); 27 changes *ḫarrāna* (`אוֹרַח`) to *gerra* (`מסילה`); 28 adds *usappīki* and has the feminine `בָּרְכִינִי` (II plural `בָּרְכוּנִי`); 29 `פנייך` (II `פניכם`); 30–33 = II 267–270 (brackets); 34 *ina maḫrīki* `לפנייך` (II "before Rīmat-Ninsun"); 48 = second half of II 262; 49–50 = II 263–264 in the 3rd person (50 *gerra*). The speech formulas of 13–14, 23, 228–229 as II 193, 216, I 273.
- **SB I**: 17 / 117 the Ninsun formula (I 257–258, 284–285) without the speech verb; 22 = I 272 (brackets); 4 `ההולך לפנים` as I 31; 210 `כתינוק פעוט` as I 253 / II 107; 1, 215 *ṭehû* as X 204.
- **Others**: 89 *ziqqu* `נשיבה` as VI 34; 97 *šubtu nēḫtu* / *mayyāl* as VII 141–142 and I 230; 214 as VII 143; 223 *nērebēt šadî* `מבואות ההרים` as X 34, 38; 173 the journey formula of IV 1 / XI 301 (imperative); 88–92 ~ V 159–163 (the execution of the winds), 93 ~ V 165 – **first copies for V**: `הָעֵר לו, שַׁמַשׁ, על חומבבה את הסופות הגדולות:` / `רוח דרום, רוח צפון, רוח קדים, רוח מערב, נשיבה, נשיבה־סוערת,` / `שַׁפַּרְזִיקּוּ, סַעַר, סִימוּרְרוּ, אַסַּכּוּ,` / `קָרָה, סוּפָה, עַלְעוֹל:` / `שלוש עשרה רוחות יקומו, ופני חומבבה יחשכו,` / `ונשקו של גילגמש יגיע אל חומבבה!` (V changes precative to preterite and redistributes the list over lines).
- **OB III (not yet translated)**: 1 ~ OB III 215; 2 ~ 249; 3 ~ 250 (different); 4 ~ 255 (*ušallim* > *ušezzeb*); 5 ~ 256 (*pagaršu* > *ibiršu*); 6 ~ 251; 8 ~ 252 (different); 223 ~ 253; 79 ~ 260–261; 166 ~ 257; 135 ~ 256. The OB III translator should start from 1–12 here and change exactly what the OB changes (decision 4 for *šullumu*).

### Proposals for the shared renderings
1. **Names (§1)**: Aya = `אַיָּה` (always vocalized; *kallatu* = `הַכַּלָּה`); Irnini = `אִירְנִינִי`; Kurnugi = `כּוּרְנוּגִי` (vocalized, note "land of no return"); Egalmaḫ `אֵגַלְמַח` (used); Apsû = `אַפְּסוּ`; Marduk `מַרְדּוּךְ`; cultic women: *ugbabtu* = `כּוֹהֶנֶת־אוּגְבַּבְתּוּ`, *kulmašītu* = `כוּלְמַשִּׁית`, *qadištu* = `קְדֵשָׁה` (§4), *mārāt ilī* = `בְּנוֹת הָאֵלִים`, *šerku* = `נָתִין` (open decision 7).
2. **Key terms (§4)**: *kallatu* = `כַּלָּה` [cognate] and *ḫīrtu* to another word (open decision 1); *qutrinnu* = `קְטוֹרֶת` [cognate], *qutrinna šakānu* = `שׂם קטורת` (Deut 33:10); *maṣṣartu* = `נְצִירָה` (pl. `נְצִירוֹת`; open decision 3) – add to the *naṣāru* row; *šalāmu* D = `הביא בשלום` (open decision 2); *paqādu* = `הִפְקִיד` [cognate] (11–12, 57); *kapāru* II "wipe" = `כִּפֵּר` [cognate] (Gen 32:21; open decision 5); *leqû ana mārūti* = `לקח לבן` (Esth 2:7), *liqûtu* = `נלקח`, *tarbûtu* = `מגודל`; *ṭēma šakānu* = `שׂם טַעַם` (Ezra 4:19); *dīna dânu* = `דן דין` (Jer 22:16); *pānī šakānu ana* = `שׂם פנים אל` (Gen 31:21); *ṣītu* = `מוֹצָא` (also "offspring"); *damāqu* D = `עשה חסד עם` (with *dumqu* = `חסד`); *burāšu* = `בְּרוֹשׁ` [cognate]; *quʾʾû* = `קִוָּה` [cognate]; *šanû* III D = `שָׁנָה` [cognate].
3. **Ordinary words (§5)**: *ṭūdu* = `נָתִיב`; *gerru* = `מְסִלָּה` (open decision 6); *takālu* = `בָּטַח` (D `הִבְטִיחַ`); *miḫiṣtu*/*meḫṣu* = `מַחַץ`; *kullumu* D stat. = `הָרְאָה`; *gimru* = `כֹּל`; *narmaku* = `מֶרְחָץ`; *bīnu* = `אֶשֶׁל`; *tullal* = `בּוֹרִית`; *raqqatu* = `(אריג) דק`; *simmiltu* = `מַדְרֵגוֹת` (used); *surqinnu* = `מנחה` (used), *nindabû* = `תְּרוּמוֹת־לֶחֶם`; *dapīnu* = `עָרִיץ`; *itû* = `גְּבוּל`; *barārītu* = `דִּמְדּוּמִים`; *ruššû* = `אדמומית`; *puqqu* = `יִחֵל`; *nepelkû* Š = `הרחיב`; *nasāqu* Št = `הכשיר`; *arraku* / *kurrû* = `ארוך` / `קצר`; *purīdu* = `פְּסִיעָה`; *karāšu* = `מַחֲנֶה`; *emēdu* N (battle) = `נתקל ב־`; the wind names of 89–91 (first copies for V 159–163; *ziqqu* = `נְשִׁיבָה` as VI 34; *šuruppû* = `קָרָה`); *eṭû* = `חָשַׁךְ`; *ḫarāpu* Š = `הקדים`; *sissiktu* = `כָּנָף`; *ebbu* = `צַח`; *uʾʾurtu* = `צַו`; *ḫaṭṭu* = `שַׁרְבִיט`; *emēqu* = `חָכַם`; *watmu* = `אֶפְרוֹחַ`; *enšu* = `חַלָּשׁ`; *nizmatu* = `מַאֲוַי`; *šaḫtu* = `נִכְנָע`; *kutallu* = `אֲחוֹרַיִם`; *lasāmu* Gtn = `מִתְרוֹצֵץ`; *saḫāru* D = `הֶחֱזִיר`.
4. **§7 "always vocalize"**: `אַיָּה` (Aya / "where?"), `גְּשָׁה`, `הָרְאָה`, `שׁוֹנָה` (repeats / different), `טַעַם` (decree / taste), `יְאַבֵּד` (Piel / Qal), `דִּין` (imperative / noun), `אַל` (vetitive / "to"), `תְּכַפֵּר` (wipe / atone).
5. **verify.py**: no change needed for III. A §3.1-style check of the winds passage (88–93 against V 159–165) would help the V translator.

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A noun derived from a reserved verb carries the reservation**: *maṣṣartu* "watch" comes from *naṣāru*, and the natural Hebrew `אַשְׁמוּרָה` would have brought the root שמר (Gen 3:24, 4:9) into the text by the back door; render the derived nouns from the same Hebrew root as the verb (`נְצִירָה`).
- **When two Akkadian words meet in one Hebrew word only in a later tablet** (*kallatu* and *ḫīrtu* in `כַּלָּה`), the cognate should win and the earlier choice be revised corpus-wide; record it as soon as it appears rather than inventing a third word locally.
- **The SB's revisions of an OB proverb are in the verbs** (*ušallim* > *ušezzeb*, *pagaršu* > *ibiršu*, *iṣṣur* > *liṣṣur* in the second copy): fix the first copy so that each verb has its own Hebrew, and the later copies show the change.

## Tablet V

### Scope
SB Tablet V, all 274 eBL labels in eBL's order (1–106, 115–187, 190–207, 247–276, 276′, 277–294, 298–308, 309a, 309b, 310–324): the arrival at the edge of the forest, the track of Ḫumbaba and "the Cedar Mountain, the dwelling of the gods, the throne-dais of Irnini" (1–16); the noisy forest of birds, crickets and monkeys (17–26, Sulaymaniyah tablet only); terror falls on Gilgameš, the heroes encourage each other ("he who goes in front guards himself", "they made a name for distant days", "one friend is one alone, two are two … a three-ply rope", 27–89); Gilgameš weeps before Šamaš, a voice from heaven: "he must not wrap himself in his seven cloaks of radiance" (90–106); Ḫumbaba insults Enkidu ("spawn of a fish, who knew not his father") and threatens Gilgameš (115–128); Enkidu urges ("to send the Deluge is to crack the whip", 129–142); the battle, the earth splits, Sirion and Lebanon are sundered, Šamaš's thirteen winds (143–165, 143–151 not attested in SB); Ḫumbaba's plea ("trees I will keep for you", 166–177); Enkidu's counsel to kill him before Enlil hears, three times (178–207, 247–275); Ḫumbaba's curse ("may the pair of them not grow old … may Enkidu have no one to bury him", 276–280); the killing (282–292); the felling of the forest and of his seven sons (300–313); the door for Enlil's temple, the raft on the Euphrates, Ḫumbaba's head (314–324). Ten manuscripts (NinNA1a–d, NinNA2, BabaNB1 = the Sulaymaniyah tablet T 1447, BabNB1, UrkNB1, two quotations); about half of the tablet lost (107–114, 188–189, 208–246, 295–297), much of the rest broken; 141, 143–151 and 184 have no SB witness (George's translation follows the MB Ugarit tablet, not available to us).

### Sources consulted
- eBL `L/1/4` SB V (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-SB-V/`): `reading-edition.md` (N, T, L, notes, ATF of every manuscript), `manuscripts.md`.
- `../parallels_for.py V --all` (130 lines with parallels); the first copies in II (193, 216, 232–233, 289), III (88–93, 4–5, 9, 164), VII (44–45, 91–92, 133), X (36, 94, 185), and the OB parallels through eBL's normalizations (OB Harmal₂ 5, 14–15, 42–48; OB Ishchali 12′–39′; OB IM 13–29; none translated yet).
- `../renderings.md` incl. §9–§15 (binding); both `project.md` files with their tablet sections; the format models (flood XI 1–60, Enūma eliš VI 1–40); `annotated-II.rtl.md` and `annotated-III.rtl.md` as the nearest models.
- George's translation (eBL T), after drafting; Tchernichovsky 1924 (`../../sources/Ben-Yehuda--Tchernichovsky-1924/full-work--all-tablets.rtl.md`, ll. 1571–1745, his Tablet V) after pass (c).
- The vocalized Bible through `../../tools/check_bible_quotes.py --show`.

### Decisions
1. **Generated from one data file per section** (scratchpad `V-gilg/V-[abcd].data.md` + `gen_V.py`), as for II, III and X: the eBL normalization is inserted by script; 276′ (N field empty) gets `[...] ... [...]` from the ATF.
2. **No garden word.** The forest is *qištu* = `יַעַר`, the poetic *ḫalbu* = `חֹרֶשׁ` (98, as VII 39), *ṣarṣaru* (124, 193) = `סְבַךְ` (Isa 10:34 `סִבְכֵי הַיַּעַר`); *šadû* = `הַר`; Lebanon and Sirāra = `הַלְּבָנוֹן`, `שִׂרְיוֹן` (156). The Akkadian has no *kirû*, so there is no `גַּן` and no `עֵדֶן` in the `>` lines; the Ezekiel 28 / 31 and Gen 2–3 comparisons are made in the apparatus and the index (tagged [equivalent] for *mūšab ilī* = Ezek 28:2 `מוֹשַׁב אֱלֹהִים`, [divergence] for the garden).
3. **The guardian's words**: *maṣṣaru* = `נוֹצֵר` (104 `נוצר היערות`, 197 `נוצר יער [הארזים]` as §3.1, 199, 266, 268, 305 `את הנוצר קטלתם` as X 36); *naṣāru* = `נָצַר` (49 `את גוייתו נצר`, 176 `אנצרה לך הדס`). No `שמר` string anywhere. *palālu* (48, "watchful") = `שׁוֹקֵד` (Jer 1:12), to keep נצר for *naṣāru*.
4. **Ḫumbaba's seven auras**: in SB V they are *sebe naḫlapātu ša namrirrī* (99) – "seven cloaks of radiance", one worn, six shed (100). *ḫalāpu* = `התעטף` / `עטוף` (as X), *naḫlaptu* = `מַעֲטֶה`, *namrīru* = `נֹגַהּ` (§4); *melammū* = `זְהָרִים` and *pulḫātu* = `אֵימוֹת` do not occur in the surviving SB V (only in George's translation of the unattested 148–149 and in OB Ishchali 12′–16′), so they are not in the `>` lines; the Gen 3:24 row is made on 99–100 (tagged [divergence]; the radiance word restored).
5. **The fixed passages and first copies**: the speech formula (`… פתח את פיו ואמר, אל … דיבר:`) as II 193 / 216 (31, 34, 73, 115, 125, 129, 178, 196, 249, 265, 281, 302, 313); 96 = VII 133 (brackets per this copy: *tukku* preserved, the rest broken); 91 = VII 92; 90 ~ VII 91 (*ibakki* `בוכה` for *unabbi* `מְיַלֵּל`); 130 = II 232, 131 = II 233; **159–165 copy III 88–93 and change exactly what the Akkadian changes**: precative > preterite (`העיר`, `קמו`, `חשכו`, `הגיעו`), the list of winds redistributed (160 ends with `נשיבה,`; 161 adds `נשיבה־סוערת,` at the head; 162 adds `אַסַּכּוּ,` at the head), *kakkū* plural `וכלי־נשקו` (as III 164); 167 ~ II 289 (`אמך ילדתך` for `לבך נשאך`); **316–317 ~ VII 44–45** (the first copy): third person `גובהה`, `רוחבה`, `עמודה, צירה ו*פותה*` for VII's second person `־ךְ`, *ēpuš-ma dalta* added, and *ištēt ammat uppuša* rendered by the lemma (*epēšu* D stative `עשויה`, as I 104 `עשוי`) instead of VII 44's *ūbu* `עובייך`.
6. **The repeated counsel** 197–204 = 266–272 (and 194–195 = 263–264; 90 / 185 / 206 / 256 / 274; 91 / 186 / 207 / 257 / 275): identical Hebrew except brackets, the Akkadian differences (266 without *erēni*; 271 = 202 + 203 in one line; 272 preserves *inarru* `קוטל`), and final marks.
7. **New renderings** (all grepped against every earlier `>` line): *napātu* `*משתאים*` (1, 53; Gen 24:21); *palāsu* Ntn `מסתכלים ומסתכלים` (no `שוב ושוב`); *alāku* Gtn `התהלך` (4, as I 151 `מתהלך`); *kibsu* `שביל`; *ḫarranātu* `האורחות` (plene, unvocalized: `הָאֳרָחוֹת` fails the strip test); *šutēšuru* `מיושר`; *ṭiābu* `טוב` (5, 9; the cognate); *parakku* `דוכן־הקודש` (I 5); *ḫiṣbu* `גֹּדֶשׁ` / *nuḫšu* `שֶׁפַע` / *ṭuḫdu* `לרוב` (three "plenty" words); *ṣillu* `צֵל` [cognate]; *giṣṣu* `נַעֲצוּץ` (not `קוץ`); *elēpu* Št `מסתבך`; *ballukku* `*בַלּוּכּוּ*`; *papallu* `שָׁתִיל` (not `נצר`); *šurmēnu* `שׁוּרְבִּין` (the Aramaic cognate; ≠ *burāšu* `ברוש`); *laʾābu* `גָּרוּב` (not `נגוע`: נגע is *lapātu*); *ḫâlu* `נזל` (Song 4:15), *ḫīlu* `שׂרף`; *zanānu* G `נִמְטַר` (15, 158; keeps the root of Š `המטיר`); *ḫarru* `אפיק`; *zizānu* `צרצר`; *šisītu* `צווחה`; *šamāru* Gtn `משורר`; *ḫalālu* Š `מַחֲלִיל` (not `מְחַלֵּל`, which collides with *sehû* D); *uršānu* (bird) `יוֹנַת־הַיַּעַר`, *sukannīnu* `תּוֹר`; *laqlaqqu* `חסידה`; *riāšu* `יָגִיל` (with *rīšātu* `גיל`); *tarlugallu* `תרנגול`; *pagû* `קוף`, *mīrānu* `גור`, *labû* `צווח`; *nāru* / *tigû* `מנגן` / `מתופף`; *mangu* `*קשיון*`; *kamālu* `זעף`; *apillû* `כוהן־אַפִּלּוּ` (the §11 pattern; vocalized, since `אפילו` = "even"); *lilissu* `תוף`; *mašû* `שכח`; *pitqudu* `זהיר`; *tākaltu* `נדן`; *lapātu* "smear" `מרוח` (41, 57; *lapātu* "touch" `נגע`, 135, 288); *ḫalāpu* G "slip into" `חמק` (60); *uttūtu* `בהלה?`; *makû* `דל`; *mušḫalṣītu* `חלקלקה`; *ašlu* `אגמון`, *šušlušu* `משולש` [cognate]; *ūṣu* `שלח` (≠ *šiltāḫu* `חץ`); *arkatu* `ירכתיים` (≠ *kutallu* `אחוריים`); *nadānu* (89; eBL *nadû*) `נתן`; *ragāmu* `רעם`; *malāku* Gt `יִימָּלְכוּ` (`נמלך ב־`); *nuʾû* `בַּעַר`; *nūnu* `דג`; *raqqu* / *šeleppû* `צב־מים` / `צב`; *enēqu* `ינק` [cognate]; *aḫû* `זר`; *napšāru* `גרון`; *ṣarṣaru* `סבך`; *nāʾeru* `שואג` (124, 193, 308); *gašriš* `ככבירים`; *nappāḫu* `נפח`, *napāḫu* `נפח` [cognate], *tumru* `רמץ`, *šalû* `*לצלול*`, *ištuḫḫu* `שוט`, *šapāru* `שלח`; *rāṭu* `רהט` (§10); *ragāmu* `רעם`; *asīdu* `עקב`; *bêšu* `זז` (§14); *sâru* `כרכר` (§13); *ḫepû* Dt `התבקע`; *ṣalāmu* `שָׁחַר`; *nakāpu* `נגף` (164, 315; law corpus); *sapānu* `שיטח`; *eṭēru* `מלט` (≠ *ezēbu* Š `הציל`); *unnīnu* `תחנונים`; *libbātu* `חרון`; *girûtu* `מריבה` (not `איבה`, Gen 3:15); *agru* `שכיר`; *šitmuru* `השתוללות`; *ekâm* `היכן`; *bâru* `לכד`; *rummû* `הרפייה` (194, 263; not `רפיון` = *luʾtu*); *labāru* D `הזקין` (279; see open decision 1); *qēbiru* `קובר`; *ḫašû* `ריאה`; *riqqu* `בושם`; *tušāru* `ישימון`; *dunnu* `עוצמה`; *sumkīnu* `*שבבים*` (Hos 8:6); *terku* `מכה`; *nikkas* `חצי קנה`; *urmazillu* `*גזעים*`; *muḫḫu* `קדקוד` (≠ *qaqqadu* `ראש`); *atmanu* `דביר`; *mūtu* … *izannun* `מוות … נמטר`.
8. **Cognates kept visible**: *erēnu* `ארז`, *iṣu* `עץ` (175, 177, 311 – every `עץ` of the tablet), *ṣillu* `צל`, *šušlušu* `משולש`, *enēqu* `ינק`, *napāḫu* `נפח`, *nappāḫu* `נפח`, *rāṭu* `רהט`, *ḫalālu* `מחליל` (חליל), *eršu* `ערש`, *šanû* D `שִׁנָּה` (42; with *ṭēmu*: `שנה את דעתך` ↔ 1 Sam 21:14 `וַיְשַׁנּוֹ אֶת טַעְמוֹ`), *ṭiābu* `טוב`, *nasāḫu* `נסח`, *rīmu* `ראם`, *šumu* `שם`, *sebe* `שבע`, *Purattu* `פרת`.
9. **The curse of Ḫumbaba (279–280)** is a precative (*ay ulabbirū*, *ai irši*), not *arāru*: no ARR string; the parallel with Gen 3:14 is tagged [divergence]. Enkidu's *adi arrāt …* (283) is the only *arratu*: rendered `קללות`, not `מארות`, because `verify.py` allows ARR only for the lemmas *arāru* / *arratu* and eBL lemmatizes *arru* "cursed" here (a slip: *ar-rat* is the construct plural of *arratu*); see Proposals 3.
10. **Lines without SB witness** (141, 143–151, 184): the `>` line is `[…]`, George's translation is quoted in the apparatus (T), and a footnote in the clean translation gives it in Hebrew for 143–151, marked as George's, from the MB Ugarit version. The parallels rows that rest on it say so.
11. **Brackets** follow the ATF of this copy: e.g. 9 `טוב` (only the first sign broken), 10 `מסתבך` (root preserved in BabaNB1), 28 `[אימה]`, 31 `[אֶנְקִידוּ פ]תח`, 53 `[עמדו]` against 1 `עמדו`, 91 `ז[ולגות …]`, 96 `תרועה [מן השמים …]` (VII 133 the reverse), 131 unbracketed (roots preserved, as II 233), 185–186 wholly bracketed (no witness), 206 / 256 / 274 per their manuscripts. Words that N puts in round brackets (in some manuscripts only) are not translated (12 *ištēn*, 30 *u*, 260 *ana*/*šā*, 270 *u*, 312 *u*), except 170 *libbi* (the only witness has it) and 310 *u* (needed for the number).
12. **eBL slips recorded**: *qišta* lemmatized *qiāšu* "give" (1, 300, 303); *ittanaplasū* as the noun *naplāsu* (2–3); *apālu* "answer" as *apālu* II "pay" (18, 21, 304); *uršānu* (a bird) as "warrior" (21); *pāšū* as *pû* "mouth" (58); *liddikku* (*nadānu*) as *nadû* (89); *arê* "eagle" as *arû* "result" (124; 193 correct); *raḫāṣu* I "trample" as *raḫāṣu* II "trust" (164, 300, 306); *aššīka* (*našû*) as *šasû* (192); *arrāt* as *arru* (283); eBL's parallels III 88–92 = V 137–141 (positional, false; the true pair is V 159–163, as the III translator noted).

### Open decisions for the user
1. **279 *labāru* D "grow old" = `אַל יזקינו`** (chosen: the natural Hebrew, and it makes the curse legible against 1 Sam 2:32 `וְלֹא יִהְיֶה זָקֵן בְּבֵיתְךָ`), although *šību* = `זָקֵן` (§5) is another lemma; alternative: `אַל יֶעְתְּקוּ` (Job 21:7 `עָתְקוּ`; flood XI 12 *labir* = `עתיקה`), which keeps *labāru* = עתק but is obscure.
2. **283 *arrāt* = `קללות`** (forced by verify.py) or `מארות` (*arratu* = `מְאֵרָה`, §4, the cognate): change after verify.py accepts the lemma *arru*.
3. **7 *irninnī* = the name `אִירְנִינִי`** (eBL's lemma; III 102) or George's generic plural "the goddesses" (`האלות`)?
4. **13, 320 *šurmēnu* = `שׁוּרְבִּין`** (Aramaic cognate, rabbinic Hebrew) or a biblical tree name (`תְּאַשּׁוּר`, Isa 60:13, identification unknown)?
5. **48 *palālu* = `שׁוֹקֵד`** and *pitqudu* = `זָהִיר` (the root פקד kept out because `מתפקד` reads "mustered")?
6. **194, 263 *rummû* = `הרפייתי`** ("my release", from *ramû* D = `הִרְפָּה`) or the plainer `שחרורי`?
7. **317 *uppuša* = `עשויה`** (eBL's lemma *epēšu*; cf. I 104) or George's "its thickness" (`עוביה`, = VII 44 *ūbu*)?
8. **42 *apillû* = `כוהן־אַפִּלּוּ`** (the §11 pattern for cultic titles) or a descriptive `מתנבא` (which would echo 1 Sam 10:10)?

### Progress log
- 2026-10-17: read the brief, the whole guidelines, corpus.md, renderings.md (incl. §9–§15), both project files with their tablet sections, the format models and annotated-II / -III; ran `parallels_for.py V --all` (130 lines); read the whole of eBL SB V (N, T, L, notes, ATF) and the Hebrew of every parallel line (II, III, VII, X); read the OB Harmal₂, Ishchali and IM normalizations for the parallel scenes.
- Drafted all 274 entries in 16 sections into four data files and generated `annotated-V.rtl.md` and `translation-V.rtl.md` (24 footnotes).
- Pass (a) fidelity: every `>` line re-read against N and the ATF. Changes: 13 `הַשּׁוּרְבִּין` (not italic: the identification is not in doubt); 45 punctuation; 82 `[שלח …]` (the rest of the line broken); 92 `אַל` vocalized; 120 `במעיי` (*ina karšīya*, not "את"); 152 `הָאַפְּ]סוּ` vocalized at its only occurrence; 205 final comma (as 255, 273); 285 `רֵעֵהוּ` vocalized (§9).
- Pass (b) consistency: the speech formulas character for character against II 193 / 216 (13 copies); 90 / 185 / 206 / 256 / 274, 91 / 186 / 207 / 257 / 275 and 197–204 / 266–272 against each other; 96 against VII 133, 130–131 against II 232–233, 159–165 against III 88–93, 167 against II 289, 316–317 against VII 44–45, 305 against X 36, 286 against X 94, 61 against X 185 – each difference justified by the Akkadian (decisions 5–6). Strip test of every vocalized word: `הָאֳרָחוֹת` > `האורחות` (unvocalized), `יִמָּלְכוּ` > `יִימָּלְכוּ`, `הַרְפָּיָתִי` > `הרפייתי`, `בִּתְּקוּ` > `בִּיתְּקוּ`, `יִגֹּף` > `יִגּוֹף`; 21 `הַתּוֹר` vocalized. Names vocalized at first occurrence (`חוּמְבָּבָה` 4, `אִירְנִינִי` 7, `גִּלְגָּמֶשׁ` 28, `אֶנְקִידוּ` 31, `אֶנְלִיל` 72, `אוּרוּךְ` 92, `אֲדַד` 106, `שִׂרְיוֹן וְהַלְּבָנוֹן` 156, `רִימַת־נִינְסוּן` 168, `נִיפּוּר`, `לַרְסָה` 202, `פְּרָת` 318); `שַׁמַשׁ`, `רֵעַ`, `בֵּרוּ`, `אוֹרַח`, `אַל` at every occurrence. Reserved strings grepped in the plain `>` lines: no `גן`, `עדן`, `שמר`, `עפר`, `טיט`, `עשב`, `נחש`, ARR, `אלהים`, `לקח`, `קרא`, `טרם`, `עולם`, `הבל`, `כנגדו`; `ידע` only for *edû* / *mūdû* (39, 117, 191); `שׂים` only *šakānu*; `שוב` only *târu* (136, 172); `עצים` only *iṣu* (175, 177, 311); `טוב` only *ṭiābu* (5, 9). Every new string grepped against all earlier `>` lines (`מתהלך` = I 151 *ittanallak*; `הדס` = VII; `רפסודה` = §11; no collisions). Speech quotation marks balanced per speech (142 and 183 close, 152 opens at the first surviving line, as II decision 13).
- Pass (c) Hebrew: read aloud; kept the literal `גושים גָּרוּב הארז`, `ברהט הנפח – נחושת לאסוף?`, `לשלוח מבול – לנגוע בשוט!`, `וגילגמש – ראש חומבבה, רוכב ו[…]`. Tchernichovsky 1924 compared (his V, ll. 1571–1745): he has `אֲשֶׁר בָּהֶן מִתְהַלֵּךְ חֻמְבַּבָּה` (our `התהלך`, independently: *alāku* Gtn), but also `וְצֵל הָעֵצִים נֶחְמָד` (Gen 2:9 `נֶחְמָד` for *ṭāb*), `מְעוֹנָה אֱלֹהֵי־הַשָּׁמַיִם`, `הַר־הָאֱלֹהִים`, `וְתַרְדֵּמָה נָפְלָה עַל אֶנְגִּידוּ` (Gen 2:21), `הָיָה לְעָפָר מִן הָאֲדָמָה` (Gen 2:7, in a dream the Akkadian of SB V does not have), `הִמְטִירוּ מָוֶת` and `פִּתְרוֹנוֹ` (Gen 40–41) – none of these in our Hebrew; ours keeps `טוב צלו`, `מושב האלים`, `נִמְטָר`.

| 317 | sāḫirša | צירה | `הַדֶּלֶת תִּסּוֹב עַל-צִירָהּ` (משלי כו:יד) | [equivalent] | = VII 45. |
| 317 | šagammaša | *פותה* | `וְהַפֹּתוֹת לְדַלְתוֹת הַבַּיִת הַפְּנִימִי לְקֹדֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים` (מלכים א ז:נ) | [equivalent] | The pivot sockets of the inner sanctum's doors. |
| 318 | ana bīt ellil ina nippuri libil purattu | אל בית אנליל בניפור ישאנה פרת | `וְהַנָּהָר הָרְבִיעִי הוּא פְרָת` (בראשית ב:יד) | [divergence] | **The Euphrates**: in Genesis it flows out of Eden; here it carries the cedar from the gods' forest to the god's temple. *purattu* restored (UrkNB1 has it). OB IM 27. |
| 318 | libil purattu | ישאנה פרת | `וַאֲנִי אֲשִׂימֵם דֹּבְרוֹת בַּיָּם עַד הַמָּקוֹם אֲשֶׁר תִּשְׁלַח אֵלַי` (מלכים א ה:כג) | [equivalent] | Cedars floated to the place of the temple. |
| 319 | atman nippuri | דבירי ניפור | `וְאֵת פֶּתַח הַדְּבִיר עָשָׂה דַּלְתוֹת` (מלכים א ו:לא) | [equivalent] | *atmanu* = דביר, the inner sanctum. |
